# CloudConnectorRegistry Class Documentation

**Namespace:** `AIAssistant`  
**File:** `cloudConnectorRegistry.h`  
**License:** GPL-3.0

## Overview

`CloudConnectorRegistry` manages a collection of cloud connectors, allowing registration, retrieval, and enumeration of connectors based on their type. Each connector implements the `ICloudConnector` interface and is uniquely identified by a string type.

## Public Methods

### void Register(std::unique_ptr<ICloudConnector> connector)

- Registers a new cloud connector.
- If a connector with the same type already exists, it will be replaced.
- Logs warnings for null or replaced connectors.
- Logs info when a connector is registered.

#### Parameters

- `connector`: `std::unique_ptr<ICloudConnector>`  
  The connector to register. Ownership is transferred to the registry.

---

### ICloudConnector* GetConnector(const std::string& type) const

- Retrieves a pointer to the registered connector of the given type.

#### Parameters

- `type`: `const std::string&`  
  The type identifier of the connector.

#### Returns

- `ICloudConnector*`  
  Pointer to the connector if found. Returns `nullptr` if not found.

---

### std::vector<std::string> GetRegisteredTypes() const

- Returns a list of all registered connector types.

#### Returns

- `std::vector<std::string>`  
  A vector containing the type identifiers of all registered connectors.

---

## Private Members

- `m_Connectors`:  
  `std::unordered_map<std::string, std::unique_ptr<ICloudConnector>>`  
  Stores registered connectors indexed by their type.

---

## Usage Example

```cpp
AIAssistant::CloudConnectorRegistry registry;
registry.Register(std::make_unique<MyConnector>());
auto connector = registry.GetConnector("MyConnectorType");
auto types = registry.GetRegisteredTypes();
```

---

## Notes

- Ownership of connectors is managed by the registry.
- Registering with a duplicate type replaces the previous connector.
- Assumes `ICloudConnector` provides a `GetType()` method returning a unique type identifier.
- Logging macros (e.g., `LOG_CORE_WARN`, `LOG_CORE_INFO`) are used for diagnostics.

---

## See Also

- `ICloudConnector` interface  
- Related logging and engine modules in the codebase


---

### 16_doc_application_cloud_cloudRetryPolicy_h

<a id="16-doc-application-cloud-cloudretrypolicy-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/16_doc_application_cloud_cloudRetryPolicy_h/docu.output.md`



# CloudRetryPolicy C++ Class Documentation

**Namespace:** `AIAssistant`  
**Header:** `cloudRetryPolicy.h`  
**License:** GPL-3.0  
**Author:** JC Technolabs (2026)

## Overview

`CloudRetryPolicy` provides a configurable retry mechanism for cloud-related operations, especially those involving HTTP requests. It uses exponential backoff with optional jitter and supports customizable retry conditions.

---

## Structures

### `Config`

Defines the retry policy parameters:

- `int m_MaxRetries`: Maximum number of retry attempts (default: 3)
- `int m_InitialBackoffMs`: Initial wait time before first retry in milliseconds (default: 1000)
- `int m_MaxBackoffMs`: Maximum wait time between retries in milliseconds (default: 30000)
- `double m_JitterFactor`: Randomization factor applied to backoff delay (+/- this factor, default: 0.2)
- `std::set<int> m_RetryableHttpCodes`: Set of HTTP status codes to retry on (default: 429, 500, 502, 503, 504)

---

### `AttemptResult`

Represents the output of an operation or HTTP request:

- `bool m_Success`: Indicates if the operation succeeded
- `int m_HttpStatusCode`: HTTP status code returned (0 if not applicable)
- `int m_RetryAfterSeconds`: Value from "Retry-After" header (seconds; 0 if not present)
- `std::string m_ErrorMessage`: Additional error message

---

## Public Methods

### `static AttemptResult Execute(const std::function<AttemptResult()>& fn, const Config& config)`

Executes a callable (e.g., lambda or function) with the specified retry policy.

- Retries if the response is unsuccessful and the HTTP status code is retryable.
- Waits based on an exponential backoff strategy with random jitter.
- If the "Retry-After" header is present, waits accordingly (up to `m_MaxBackoffMs`).
- Returns the last `AttemptResult` (success or final failure).
- Stops early for non-retryable HTTP status codes.

**Usage example:**
```cpp
CloudRetryPolicy::Config cfg; // Optionally customize cfg
auto result = CloudRetryPolicy::Execute([]() {
    // Your operation, returning CloudRetryPolicy::AttemptResult
}, cfg);
```

---

### `static AttemptResult Execute(const std::function<AttemptResult()>& fn)`

Same as above, using the default configuration.

**Usage example:**
```cpp
auto result = CloudRetryPolicy::Execute([]() {
    // Your operation, returning CloudRetryPolicy::AttemptResult
});
```

---

## Private Methods

### `static int ComputeBackoffMs(int attempt, const Config& config)`

Computes the backoff time for the given attempt.  
- Uses `initialBackoffMs * 2^attempt`.
- Applies jitter in the range `[1 - jitterFactor, 1 + jitterFactor]`.
- Ensures value does not exceed `m_MaxBackoffMs`.

---

## Behavior Summary

1. Calls the user-supplied function for up to `m_MaxRetries + 1` times.
2. If `m_Success == true`, returns immediately.
3. If the result has a non-retryable HTTP status code, stops and returns.
4. Waits before retrying using exponential backoff, jitter, and respects "Retry-After".
5. Logs each retry attempt.
6. Returns the result of the last attempt.

---

## Typical Use Case

Use `CloudRetryPolicy` to run operations that may fail intermittently due to network/server errors, such as cloud API requests, providing robustness against transient faults.

---

## Thread Safety

- Jitter calculation uses `thread_local` PRNG for safe concurrent use.

---

## Dependencies

- `<functional>`, `<set>`, `<string>`, `<random>`, `<algorithm>`, `<chrono>`, `<thread>`
- Logging macro/function `LOG_APP_INFO`

---

## License

GPL-3.0 (see source code for full details)


---

### 17_doc_application_cloud_cloudTaskExecutor_h

<a id="17-doc-application-cloud-cloudtaskexecutor-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/17_doc_application_cloud_cloudTaskExecutor_h/docu.output.md`



# Documentation: `AIAssistant::ICloudTaskExecutor` (C++)

## Overview

`ICloudTaskExecutor` is an abstract base class for implementing *cloud* task executors within the AIAssistant workflow engine. It streamlines execution of workflow tasks that operate via remote cloud services by standardizing connection resolution, credential lookup, and error handling. Implementers provide actual cloud operations through the `ExecuteCloud` method.

## Inheritance

- Inherits from: `ITaskExecutor`

## Main Responsibilities

- Resolves the required cloud connection and credentials for a given workflow task.
- Performs template expansion on input parameters.
- Checks circuit breakers to safeguard against repeated failures.
- Audits and logs critical actions and security-relevant events.
- Provides helpers for file path validation and cloud output writing.

## Key Methods

### Constructor

```cpp
ICloudTaskExecutor(CloudConnectorRegistry& connectorRegistry, CloudConnectionManager& connectionManager);
```
Initializes the executor with registries/managers for connectors and cloud connections.

### Destructor

Virtual default destructor.

### bool Execute(WorkflowDefinition const&, WorkflowRun&, TaskDef const&, TaskInstanceState&) override

- Core entry point.
- Handles:
    - Parsing task parameters (expects `"connection"` field specifying connection to use).
    - Resolving connection and connector.
    - Credential resolution via connector.
    - Template expansion for input values in params JSON.
    - Circuit breaker checks for connection.
    - Delegates actual work to `ExecuteCloud`.
    - Records circuit breaker outcome.
    - Logs errors/warnings if any step fails, updating `taskState` accordingly.
- Returns `true` on task success; `false` on failure.

### virtual bool ExecuteCloud(..., CloudConnection const&, CloudCredentials const&, TaskCancellationToken const&) = 0

- **Pure virtual.**  
- Called by `Execute` after resolving connection/credentials/circuit breaker/cancelling.
- Implemented by subclasses to perform the actual cloud operation.  
- Receives all necessary workflow/task state, connection information, credentials, and a cancellation token.

### static bool ValidateLocalPath(const std::string& localPath, const std::filesystem::path& baseDir, const std::string& taskId)

- Checks that `localPath` (relative to `baseDir`) does not escape `baseDir`.
- Blocks patterns with "`..`" or any path resolving outside `baseDir`.
- Logs a security event if an invalid path is detected.
- Returns `true` if the path is safe; `false` otherwise.

### static std::string ResponseJsonFilename(const TaskInstanceState& taskState)

- Determines the response filename for the given task instance.
- For regular tasks: `"response.json"`.
- For per-item child tasks with IDs like "`parentId#N`": `"response_<N>.json"`.

### static void WriteResponseJson(const std::filesystem::path& workDir, const TaskInstanceState& taskState, const std::string& responseBody)

- Writes the cloud response JSON string to the appropriate response file within `workDir` for the task instance.
- Creates the directory if it does not exist.
- Does nothing if `workDir` is empty or cannot be created.

## Protected Members

- `CloudConnectorRegistry& m_ConnectorRegistry`  
    Registry for available cloud connector types.
- `CloudConnectionManager& m_ConnectionManager`  
    Manages configuration/state of named cloud connections.

## Usage Approach

- To add a new cloud executor:  
    - Subclass `ICloudTaskExecutor`.
    - Implement `ExecuteCloud`.
- Use `Execute` as the entry to perform fully-managed cloud task workflow execution.

## Security

- Validates all local file paths to prevent path traversal.
- Uses circuit breakers to prevent hammering failing cloud connections.
- Logs all relevant error, warning, and security/authentication events.

## License

GPL-3.0 License.  
Copyright (c) 2026 JC Technolabs.

---

**For implementers:**  
Implement only `ExecuteCloud` to define cloud-side logic; the base class handles all preparation, validation, and bookkeeping.


---

### 18_doc_application_cloud_dbQueryCloudTaskExecutor_h

<a id="18-doc-application-cloud-dbquerycloudtaskexecutor-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/18_doc_application_cloud_dbQueryCloudTaskExecutor_h/docu.output.md`



# DbQueryCloudTaskExecutor

**Namespace:** `AIAssistant`  
**Inherits:** `ICloudTaskExecutor`  
**Purpose:** Executes PostgreSQL SQL queries in cloud workflows and writes results to disk in CSV or JSON format.

---

## Overview

`DbQueryCloudTaskExecutor` is a task executor for the JCWF workflow engine, handling tasks of type `"db_query"`. It connects to a named PostgreSQL database, executes a supplied SQL query, and saves the results as a file in the task working directory. Results may be formatted as CSV or JSON.

---

## Task Type

- **Type:** `"db_query"`

---

## Supported Task Parameters (JSON)

| Key           | Type     | Description                                                      | Required | Default                |
| ------------- | -------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- | -------- | ---------------------- |
| `connection`  | string   | Name of the preconfigured cloud/PostgreSQL connection            | Yes      | —                      |
| `query`       | string   | SQL query to execute (sent as-is to PostgreSQL)                  | Yes      | —                      |
| `format`      | string   | Output format: `"csv"` or `"json"`                               | No       | `"csv"`                |
| `output_file` | string   | Output file name (relative to task working dir)                  | No       | `result.csv`/`result.json` |

---

## Behavior

1. **Parameter Parsing:**  
   - Reads and validates `connection`, `query`, `format`, and `output_file` parameters from the task's JSON configuration.

2. **PostgreSQL Connection:**  
   - Uses libpq to establish a connection with credentials and connection info provided in the workflow.

3. **Query Execution:**  
   - Executes the specified SQL query using PostgreSQL.
   - On error, fails the task and captures error messages.

4. **Result Writing:**  
   - Results are written to disk in the selected output format:
     - **CSV:** Field values escaped per RFC 4180; NULLs written as empty fields.
     - **JSON:** Each row becomes a JSON object; NULLs become `null`.

5. **Output File:**  
   - Path is resolved relative to the workflow and working directory.
   - Defaults to `result.csv` or `result.json` if `output_file` is not set.

6. **Status Reporting:**  
   - On success, updates the task state with a short summary (number of rows, columns, format, and filename).
   - On failure, records the error message and marks the task as failed.

---

## Error Handling

- Missing or invalid parameters will fail the task.
- Database connection errors, query errors, file write errors all cause task failure with a descriptive error message.

---

## Security Notes

- SQL is sent as-is; the workflow author is responsible for correctness and avoiding injection or privileged operations.

---

## Example Task JSON

```json
{
  "task_type": "db_query",
  "params": {
    "connection": "main_db",
    "query": "SELECT id, name FROM users WHERE active=true",
    "format": "json",
    "output_file": "active_users.json"
  }
}
```

---

## See Also

- Interface: `ICloudTaskExecutor`
- PostgreSQL Client Library: [libpq](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/libpq.html)
- Related: `PostgresConnector`, `TaskPathResolver`

---

## License

GPL-3.0  
Copyright (c) 2026 JC Technolabs


---

### 19_doc_application_cloud_emailCloudTaskExecutor_h

<a id="19-doc-application-cloud-emailcloudtaskexecutor-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/19_doc_application_cloud_emailCloudTaskExecutor_h/docu.output.md`



# EmailCloudTaskExecutor C++ Class Documentation

**Namespace:** `AIAssistant`  
**Header:** `cloud/emailCloudTaskExecutor.h`  
**License:** GPL-3.0  
**Copyright:** © 2026 JC Technolabs

## Purpose

`EmailCloudTaskExecutor` implements cloud-based email operations as workflow tasks, supporting sending and reading emails via SMTP and IMAP (using libcurl). Typical use is for automation by human or AI workflow agents.

## Supported Task Types

- **email_send** — Send email(s) with optional attachments.
- **email_read** — Fetch email summary data from an IMAP server.

---

## Usage

The class is intended to be used by a workflow execution engine that manages `CloudTaskExecutor` instances to process tasks defined as JSON objects.

## Class Inheritance

`EmailCloudTaskExecutor`  
: Publicly inherits from `ICloudTaskExecutor`.

---

## API Summary

### Public Inherited Members

- All constructors and members from `ICloudTaskExecutor`.

---

### Protected Methods

#### `bool ExecuteCloud(...) override`

Implements main execution logic for email tasks. Routes to either `email_send` or `email_read` logic, depending on the task type.

- **Parameters:**
  - `workflowDefinition`, `workflowRun`, `taskDefinition`, `taskState` — workflow/task data.
  - `connection` — `CloudConnection` object with connection/auth details.
  - `credentials` — `CloudCredentials`.
  - `cancellationToken` — token for cancellation.

- **Returns:**  
  `true` if the task succeeds; `false` on error.

---

### Private Methods

#### `bool ExecuteEmailRead(...)`

Executes the "email_read" task to fetch emails from an IMAP source.

- **Parameters:**  
  Similar to above; specific to reading.

- **Returns:**  
  `true` if fetching and output succeeded.

---

## Task Types and Parameters

### email_send

Sends an email via SMTP.

- **Parameters (JSON):**
  - `connection` _(required)_: String, named SMTP connection.
  - `to` _(required)_: Recipient email(s), comma-separated.
  - `subject` _(required)_: Email subject text.
  - `body` _(required if body_file not set)_: Plain text message body.
  - `body_file` _(optional)_: Read email body from this file (overrides `body` if set).
  - `cc` _(optional)_: CC recipients, comma-separated.
  - `attachments` _(optional)_: Array of filenames (relative to task working directory).

### email_read

Fetches recent emails from an IMAP folder.

- **Parameters (JSON):**
  - `connection` _(required)_: Named IMAP connection.
  - `folder` _(optional)_: IMAP folder/subfolder (default: `"INBOX"`).
  - `max_messages` _(optional)_: Max messages to fetch (default: `10`).
  - `subject_filter` _(optional)_: Only include messages with subject matching this substring.

- **Outputs:**
  - `emails_summary.json` — Array of fetched messages (fields: `uid`, `from`, `to`, `subject`, `date`, `body`).
  - `response.json` — Task result info (`ok`, `count`, `folder`).

---

## Email Features

- **SMTP:** Builds MIME/RFC822 compliant messages, supports text body and multiple attachments (base64).
- **IMAP:** Uses SEARCH and FETCH commands to gather headers and body.
- **Connection Details:** Pulled from `CloudConnection` objects (server, port, auth info).

---

## Implementation Notes

- Uses `libcurl` for SMTP and IMAP.
- Uses OpenSSL for base64 encoding of attachments.
- Output files (`emails_summary.json`, `response.json`) are written to the working directory resolved by the workflow/task.
- Supports basic filtering (by subject) and message limiting.
- Attaches error messages in `taskState.m_LastErrorMessage` and updates `taskState.m_State` on failure.

---

## Example Task Definitions

### Sending Email

```json
{
  "type": "email_send",
  "params": {
    "connection": "work_smtp",
    "to": "user@example.com",
    "subject": "Notice",
    "body": "This is an automated email.",
    "attachments": ["report.pdf"]
  }
}
```

### Reading Email

```json
{
  "type": "email_read",
  "params": {
    "connection": "work_imap",
    "folder": "INBOX",
    "max_messages": 3,
    "subject_filter": "Notice"
  }
}
```

---

## Error Handling

- On JSON parse failure or missing/invalid parameters, returns `false` and stores error in task state.
- Logs relevant info using `LOG_APP_INFO` and `LOG_APP_WARN`.

---

## Dependencies

- `libcurl` (SMTP/IMAP)
- `simdjson` (fast JSON parsing)
- `OpenSSL` (base64 encoding)

---

## Summary

`EmailCloudTaskExecutor` is a cloud task executor class for automated sending and fetching of email as part of orchestrated workflows. It routes and executes "email_send" and "email_read" tasks, handles errors gracefully, and integrates tightly with workflow task and connection abstractions.


---

### 20_doc_application_cloud_emailConnector_h

<a id="20-doc-application-cloud-emailconnector-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/20_doc_application_cloud_emailConnector_h/docu.output.md`



# EmailConnector Class Documentation

**Namespace:** `AIAssistant`  
**Header:** `cloud/emailConnector.h`  
**Implements:** `ICloudConnector`  
**Purpose:**  
Connects to email services via SMTP (send) and IMAP (read) using libcurl. Supports credential retrieval from a key manager and parameterized cloud connection configuration.

---

## Overview

The `EmailConnector` class provides methods for:

- Authenticating via username/password from a credential provider.
- Testing SMTP connectivity.
- Constructing libcurl-style SMTP and IMAP URLs.
- Executing IMAP commands.
- Parsing IMAP SEARCH responses for UIDs.
- Polling IMAP folders for new mail efficiently.

---

## Parameters

`CloudConnection.m_Params` keys:
- `smtp_host` (string, required for SMTP): SMTP server host, e.g., `smtp.gmail.com`.
- `smtp_port` (string, optional): SMTP port. Default is `587` (STARTTLS), or `465` (SSL).
- `imap_host` (string, required for IMAP): IMAP server host, e.g., `imap.gmail.com`.
- `imap_port` (string, optional): IMAP port. Default is `993`.
- `from` (string, optional): Sender email address. Default is credential username.
- `use_ssl` (string, optional): `"true"` (default) or `"false"`.

Credentials obtained from `CloudConnection.m_KeyName` (points to a key manager entry).  
`CloudConnection.m_AuthType` must be `BasicAuth`.

---

## Public Methods

### `std::string GetType() const`

Returns the connector type ("email").

---

### `bool TestConnection(const CloudConnection& connection, std::string& errorMessage)`

Tests SMTP connection and authentication via libcurl.  
**Parameters:**  
- `connection`: Cloud connection parameters and credentials.
- `errorMessage`: Set on failure.

**Returns:** `true` if connection and authentication succeed, `false` otherwise.

---

### `bool ResolveCredentials(const CloudConnection& connection, CloudCredentials& credentials, std::string& errorMessage)`

Gets credentials (username, password) from the key manager.  
**Parameters:**  
- `connection`: Connection parameters.
- `credentials`: Populated with username, password, and auth type.
- `errorMessage`: Set on failure.

**Returns:** `true` on success, `false` if key is missing or invalid.

---

### `static std::string BuildSmtpUrl(const CloudConnection& connection)`

Builds an SMTP URL for libcurl.  
**Example Output:**  
- `smtps://smtp.gmail.com:465`
- `smtp://smtp.gmail.com:587`

**Uses:** `smtp_host`, `smtp_port`, and `use_ssl`.

---

### `static std::string BuildImapUrl(const CloudConnection& connection)`

Builds an IMAP URL for libcurl.  
**Example Output:**  
- `imaps://imap.gmail.com:993`

**Uses:** `imap_host`, `imap_port`, and `use_ssl`.

---

### `static bool ImapCommand(const std::string& url, const std::string& username, const std::string& password, const std::string& customRequest, std::string& responseBody, std::string& errorMessage, bool useSsl)`

Executes an IMAP command via libcurl and returns the response.  
**Parameters:**  
- `url`: IMAP URL (including folder).
- `username`: IMAP account.
- `password`: Credential password.
- `customRequest`: IMAP command (e.g., `SEARCH ALL`).
- `responseBody`: Receives server response.
- `errorMessage`: Set on failure.
- `useSsl`: Whether to use SSL.

**Returns:** `true` on success.

---

### `static std::vector<std::string> ParseSearchUids(const std::string& searchResponse)`

Extracts message UIDs from an IMAP SEARCH response.  
**Input Example:** `* SEARCH 1 2 3\r\n`

**Returns:** Vector of UID strings.

---

### `static std::string CheckForNewMail(const CloudConnection& connection, const CloudCredentials& credentials, const std::string& folder, const std::string& subjectFilter, const std::string& lastSeenUid, bool& hasNewMail, std::string& errorMessage)`

Scans an IMAP folder for messages newer than `lastSeenUid`.  
**Parameters:**  
- `connection`: Connection info.
- `credentials`: Resolved credentials.
- `folder`: IMAP folder name.
- `subjectFilter`: Optional, only messages with this subject.
- `lastSeenUid`: Watermark UID from last poll.
- `hasNewMail`: Receives `true` if new mail is found.
- `errorMessage`: Set on error.

**Returns:**  
- The highest UID found on success.
- If `lastSeenUid` is empty (first call), seeds the watermark (does not report existing mail as new).
- Returns empty string on failure.

---

## Usage Notes

- Relies on libcurl for all network operations.
- Requires credentials to be stored with the key manager as username/password (or app password).
- Both SMTP and IMAP require the correct server host, port, and SSL parameters.
- Notifies of new mail by comparing IMAP UIDs since the last poll.

---

## Licensing

GPL-3.0. See file header for details.


---

### 21_doc_application_cloud_gcsCloudTaskExecutor_h

<a id="21-doc-application-cloud-gcscloudtaskexecutor-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/21_doc_application_cloud_gcsCloudTaskExecutor_h/docu.output.md`



# GcsCloudTaskExecutor C++ Class Documentation

## Overview

`AIAssistant::GcsCloudTaskExecutor` is a task executor class for handling Google Cloud Storage (GCS) file upload and download operations as part of a workflow. It implements the `ICloudTaskExecutor` interface, enabling integration into workflow systems that require reliable cloud file transfers.

---

## Purpose

- Automates uploading and downloading files between the local filesystem and Google Cloud Storage.
- Used in workflow tasks of type `"gcs_upload"` or `"gcs_download"`.

---

## Supported Operations

- **Upload**: Transfers a local file to a specified GCS bucket/object.
- **Download**: Retrieves an object from GCS into a specified local file path.

---

## Configuration

### Task Parameters (as JSON keys)

| Key           | Required | Description                                                                         |
|---------------|----------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| connection    | Yes      | Named `CloudConnection` object to use for GCS authentication and endpoint info.     |
| bucket        | No       | GCS bucket name. If omitted, uses the connection's default bucket.                  |
| object_name   | Yes      | Target object path within the bucket.                                               |
| local_path    | Yes      | Local path for file upload/download, relative to the working directory.             |
| operation     | Yes      | `"upload"` or `"download"` (specifies the type of operation).                       |

---

## Main Method

### ExecuteCloud

#### Signature

```cpp
bool ExecuteCloud(
    WorkflowDefinition const& workflowDefinition,
    WorkflowRun& workflowRun,
    TaskDef const& taskDefinition,
    TaskInstanceState& taskState,
    CloudConnection const& connection,
    CloudCredentials const& credentials,
    TaskCancellationToken const& cancellationToken
) override;
```

#### Description

Performs the upload or download operation by:

1. Parsing task parameters from JSON.
2. Validating required fields (`operation`, `object_name`, `local_path`, `bucket`).
3. Using the provided cloud `connection` and `credentials` for authentication.
4. For **uploads**:
   - Reads the local file.
   - Sends a POST request to GCS using the JSON API.
5. For **downloads**:
   - Ensures the output directory exists.
   - Fetches the object using a GET request and writes it to the local file.
6. Handles URL encoding of object names.
7. Captures any error messages in `taskState`.
8. Records operation results and status.

---

## Helper Functions

- **GcsRequest**: Executes a generic HTTP request (GET/POST) against the GCS API with bearer token authentication.
- **GcsDownload**: Downloads a GCS object to a local file, handling errors and size limits.
- **UrlEncode**: Safely encodes object names for HTTP requests.

---

## Error Handling

- All failures (e.g., missing parameters, file access errors, HTTP errors) set the `taskState.m_LastErrorMessage` and mark the task as failed (`TaskInstanceStateKind::Failed`).

---

## Logging

- Logs both upload and download operations, showing source and destination paths.

---

## Usage Example (Pseudocode)

```json
{
  "connection": "my_gcp_connection",
  "bucket": "example_bucket",
  "object_name": "data/report.csv",
  "local_path": "reports/report.csv",
  "operation": "upload"
}
```

---

## Notes

- Requires valid GCS credentials (bearer token) in the connection.
- Supports file sizes up to 256 MB for downloads.
- Uses curl and simdjson for networking and JSON parsing.
- Ensures the working directory structure is created as needed for downloads.

---

## License

GPL-3.0 (JC Technolabs, 2026)

---


---

### 22_doc_application_cloud_gcsConnector_h

<a id="22-doc-application-cloud-gcsconnector-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/22_doc_application_cloud_gcsConnector_h/docu.output.md`



# GcsConnector C++ Class Documentation

## Overview

`GcsConnector` is a native connector for Google Cloud Storage (GCS) that uses the JSON API and service account JWT authentication. It implements the `ICloudConnector` interface and is part of the `AIAssistant` namespace.

- Authenticates using Google service account private key (PEM) with JWT (RS256).
- Exchanges JWT for OAuth2 access token, or uses JWT directly for local/fake GCS environments.
- Provides token caching and endpoint handling.
- Relies on external dependencies: cURL, simdjson, cloudConnector, jwtGenerator, keyManager.

## Usage

Configure a `CloudConnection` object with the required parameters:

- `"bucket"`: Default GCS bucket name (string, required).
- `"service_account_email"`: Service account email (string, required).
- `m_KeyName`: Name of the credential in KeyManager (must be a `KeyPairCredential` with PEM private key).
- `m_Endpoint` (optional): GCS API base URL. Default is `https://storage.googleapis.com`. For testing: `http://localhost:4443`.
- `m_AuthType`: Must be `JwtRsa`.

## Public Methods

### `std::string GetType() const override`

Returns the connector type, `"gcs"`.

---

### `bool TestConnection(const CloudConnection& connection, std::string& errorMessage) override`

Checks connectivity to the configured GCS bucket.

- Validates required parameters.
- Resolves credentials.
- Performs a `GET /storage/v1/b/{bucket}` request.
- Returns `true` on success.
- Returns `false` with `errorMessage` on failure.

---

### `bool ResolveCredentials(const CloudConnection& connection, CloudCredentials& credentials, std::string& errorMessage) override`

Resolves and caches OAuth2 credentials for GCS API calls.

- Validates the presence of key name and service account email.
- Generates a signed JWT for service account.
- Exchanges JWT for OAuth2 access token unless using a local/fake GCS endpoint.
- Caches the access token.
- On success, fills the `credentials` struct and returns `true`.
- On error, returns `false` with `errorMessage`.

---

### `static std::string BuildEndpointUrl(const CloudConnection& connection)`

Builds the effective GCS API endpoint URL.

- Returns `m_Endpoint` from `CloudConnection` if set, otherwise returns the default GCS endpoint.
- Trims any trailing "/" from the base URL.

---

## Authentication Flow

1. Retrieve the private key PEM from KeyManager.
2. Generate JWT with:
   - `iss` and `sub`: service account email
   - `aud`: `https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token`
   - `scope`: `https://www.googleapis.com/auth/devstorage.read_write`
   - `iat`/`exp`: current time and expiry
3. For production, exchange JWT for OAuth2 access token.
   - HTTP POST to `https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token`
   - Content-Type: `application/x-www-form-urlencoded`
   - Use the access_token from JSON response.
4. For non-production/fake endpoints, JWT is used directly as Bearer token.
5. Tokens are cached (per key) and refreshed 5 minutes before expiry.

---

## Internal Structures

### `struct CachedToken`

- `std::string m_AccessToken;`
- `std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point m_ExpiresAt;`

Stores cached OAuth2 access tokens and their expiry.

### Private Members

- `mutable std::mutex m_CacheMutex;`
- `mutable std::unordered_map<std::string, CachedToken> m_TokenCache;`

Token cache is thread-safe.

---

## Dependencies

- [cURL](https://curl.se/)
- [simdjson](https://github.com/simdjson/simdjson)
- Internal: `cloudConnector.h`, `core.h`, `engine.h`, `jwtGenerator.h`, `keyManager.h`, `curlWrapper.h`

---

## Error Handling

All methods that may fail will set an error message in the provided `errorMessage` output parameter.

---

## Licensing

GNU General Public License v3.0 (GPL-3.0)
JC Technolabs, 2026

---

## Example

```cpp
AIAssistant::CloudConnection conn;
conn.m_Name = "myGcs";
conn.m_Params["bucket"] = "my-bucket";
conn.m_Params["service_account_email"] = "svc-account@example.com";
conn.m_Endpoint = ""; // Use default endpoint
conn.m_KeyName = "gcs-key";
conn.m_AuthType = CloudAuthType::JwtRsa;

AIAssistant::GcsConnector gcs;
std::string error;

if (gcs.TestConnection(conn, error)) {
    // Connection is valid
} else {
    // error contains reason
}
```

---

## Thread Safety

Token cache access is protected by a mutex.

---

## Notes

- Requires a Google service account JSON key loaded into `KeyManager` as a `KeyPairCredential` containing the PEM private key and email.
- Valid for both production and local testing (fake-gcs-server).
- Does not manage bucket creation, only authentication and bucket access testing.

---


---

### 23_doc_application_cloud_gitHubCloudTaskExecutor_h

<a id="23-doc-application-cloud-githubcloudtaskexecutor-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/23_doc_application_cloud_gitHubCloudTaskExecutor_h/docu.output.md`



# GitHubCloudTaskExecutor C++ Class Documentation

## Overview

**`GitHubCloudTaskExecutor`** implements automated GitHub issue operations via the GitHub REST API.  
It is designed for integration with cloud-based workflows, supporting standard GitHub issue operations using task parameters defined in JSON.  

- **Namespace:** `AIAssistant`
- **Inheritance:** Inherits from `ICloudTaskExecutor`
- **Purpose:** Enables programmatic management of GitHub issues, comments, and repository files within automated workflows.

## Supported Operations

Set using the `"operation"` JSON key:

- `"create"`: Create a new GitHub issue.
- `"comment"`: Add a comment to an issue or PR.
- `"close"`: Close an issue or PR.
- `"get_file"`: Get the content of a file from a repository.
- `"list_issues"`: List open issues in a repository.

## Task Parameters (JSON Keys)

| Key             | Type          | Required | Used In (operation) | Description                                 |
|-----------------|--------------|----------|---------------------|---------------------------------------------|
| `connection`    | string       | Yes      | all                 | Name of the CloudConnection to use.         |
| `operation`     | string       | Yes      | all                 | Operation type.                             |
| `owner`         | string       | No       | all                 | GitHub repo owner. Uses connection default if omitted. |
| `repo`          | string       | No       | all                 | Repository name. Uses connection default if omitted.    |
| `title`         | string       | Yes      | create              | Title of the new issue.                     |
| `body`          | string       | Varies   | create, comment     | Body text for issue or comment.             |
| `labels`        | array<string>| No       | create              | Labels for the issue.                       |
| `issue_number`  | string/int   | Yes      | comment, close      | Issue or PR number.                         |
| `path`          | string       | Yes      | get_file            | File path in the repository.                |
| `ref`           | string       | No       | get_file            | Git ref (branch/commit/sha). Default: main/master.      |

## Key Method

#### `bool ExecuteCloud(...) override`

**Purpose:** Executes the specified GitHub API operation according to task parameters.

**Parameters:**
- `workflowDefinition` : Workflow definition context.
- `workflowRun` : Workflow execution state.
- `taskDefinition` : Task details (including input JSON).
- `taskState` : State, status, and error reporting for task.
- `connection` : Cloud connection configuration.
- `credentials` : Auth token.
- `cancellationToken` : Task cancellation/timeout support.

**Returns:**  
- `true` if the operation succeeded.  
- `false` if there was an error (see `taskState` for error message).

## Behavior Per Operation

- **create:**  
  POSTs to `/repos/{owner}/{repo}/issues`.  
  Requires `title`. Optionally adds `body` and `labels`.

- **comment:**  
  POSTs to `/repos/{owner}/{repo}/issues/{issue_number}/comments`.  
  Requires `issue_number` and `body`.

- **close:**  
  PATCHes to `/repos/{owner}/{repo}/issues/{issue_number}` with state `"closed"`.  
  Requires `issue_number`.

- **get_file:**  
  GETs `/repos/{owner}/{repo}/contents/{path}`.  
  Optionally uses `ref`.  
  Base64-decodes content and saves file in the task working directory.

- **list_issues:**  
  GETs `/repos/{owner}/{repo}/issues?state=open&per_page=100`.

## Error Handling

- Fails task with descriptive error in `taskState.m_LastErrorMessage` if:
  - Required parameters are missing.
  - HTTP response indicates failure (HTTP ≥ 400).
  - JSON parsing or network failures.

## Notes

- Limits response capture for stdout to 1024 bytes (`kMaxCaptureChars`).
- Writes full GitHub HTTP response to the task working directory as JSON.
- Uses `libcurl` for REST calls, `simdjson` for JSON parsing, and OpenSSL BIO for Base64 decoding.

## Example Task JSON Input

```json
{
  "connection": "main-gh",
  "operation": "create",
  "owner": "octocat",
  "repo": "Hello-World",
  "title": "Bug report",
  "body": "There is a problem with...",
  "labels": ["bug", "assistant"]
}
```

## License

GPL-3.0 (see source code for details)  
© 2026 JC Technolabs


---

### 24_doc_application_cloud_gitHubConnector_h

# GitHubConnector Class Documentation

**Namespace:** `AIAssistant`

## Overview

`GitHubConnector` is a cloud connector class implementing the `ICloudConnector` interface. It enables interaction with GitHub, GitLab, or GitHub Enterprise repositories via REST API using Bearer token (Personal Access Token, PAT) authentication.  

## Usage

- Intended for cloud connections with GitHub/GitLab using secure token authentication.
- Requires a configured `CloudConnection` object specifying the endpoint and repository details.
- Uses credentials managed by a `KeyManager`.

## Configuration Parameters

| Parameter              | Description                                                      |
|------------------------|------------------------------------------------------------------|
| `m_Params["owner"]`    | Default repository owner/organization                            |
| `m_Params["repo"]`     | Default repository name                                          |
| `m_Endpoint`           | API base URL (default: `https://api.github.com`)                 |
|                        | For GitLab: `https://gitlab.com/api/v4`                         |
|                        | For GitHub Enterprise: e.g., `https://github.mycompany.com/api/v3` |
| `m_KeyName`            | Credential name in the `KeyManager` (should provide a PAT)       |
| `m_AuthType`           | Must be `BearerToken`                                            |

## Key Members

### Static Constants

- `DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL`  
  Default API endpoint: `"https://api.github.com"`

### Methods

#### `std::string GetType() const override`

Returns the connector type identifier: `"github"`.

#### `bool TestConnection(const CloudConnection& connection, std::string& errorMessage) override`

Tests whether the credentials and endpoint in `connection` can successfully authenticate and access the GitHub API.  
- On success: returns `true`.  
- On failure: returns `false`, with error information in `errorMessage`.

#### `bool ResolveCredentials(const CloudConnection& connection, CloudCredentials& credentials, std::string& errorMessage) override`

Resolves and validates credentials for the given connection.  
- On success: fills `credentials` (sets `BearerToken` and token value), returns `true`.  
- On failure: returns `false`, with error details.

#### `static std::string GetApiBaseUrl(const CloudConnection& connection)`

Returns the API base URL for the given `CloudConnection`. Uses provided endpoint, or default if not specified.

## Authentication

- Uses `BearerToken` authentication.
- Requires a Personal Access Token (PAT) stored in `KeyManager`.

## Example Workflow

1. `ResolveCredentials`: Checks for a credential key, fetches PAT from `KeyManager`, validates presence.
2. `TestConnection`:  
   - Uses PAT to send a `GET /user` request to the API endpoint.
   - Checks HTTP response status.
   - Error details are provided for invalid credentials or network failures.

## Dependencies

- Requires [libcurl](https://curl.se/libcurl/) for HTTP requests.
- Uses `simdjson` for potential JSON handling.
- Depends on `cloud/cloudConnector.h`, `core.h`, `engine.h`, `keyManager.h`, and `curlWrapper.h`.

## Error Handling

- Provides detailed error messages for missing credentials, invalid tokens, or HTTP errors.
- Returns error conditions explicitly via return values and `errorMessage`.

## License

GPL-3.0.  
See source code header for legal details.


---

### 25_doc_application_cloud_googleSheetsCloudTaskExecutor_h

<a id="25-doc-application-cloud-googlesheetscloudtaskexecutor-h"></a>

# GoogleSheetsCloudTaskExecutor

**Namespace**: `AIAssistant`  
**Inheritance**: `ICloudTaskExecutor`

## Overview

`GoogleSheetsCloudTaskExecutor` executes Google Sheets read and write tasks via the Google Sheets API v4 as part of a workflow system. It is designed for integration with a cloud-oriented task framework, enabling reading from and writing to Google Sheets as task steps.

## Supported Task Types

- `sheets_read`: Reads a cell range from a Google Sheets spreadsheet.
- `sheets_write`: Writes data to a cell range in a Google Sheets spreadsheet.

## Parameters

### For `sheets_read`

| Parameter           | Type     | Required | Description                                                      |
|---------------------|----------|----------|------------------------------------------------------------------|
| `connection`        | string   | Yes      | Named `CloudConnection` object with credentials/config.           |
| `spreadsheet_id`    | string   | No       | Target spreadsheet ID (overrides connection param, if provided).  |
| `range`             | string   | Yes      | Cell range in A1 notation (e.g., `"Sheet1!A1:D100"`).            |
| `output_format`     | string   | No       | `"csv"` (default) or `"json"`.                                   |
| `output_file`       | string   | No       | Output file name (default: `"result.csv"` or `"result.json"`).    |

### For `sheets_write`

| Parameter              | Type     | Required | Description                                                    |
|------------------------|----------|----------|----------------------------------------------------------------|
| `connection`           | string   | Yes      | Named `CloudConnection` object with credentials/config.         |
| `spreadsheet_id`       | string   | No       | Target spreadsheet ID (overrides connection param, if provided).|
| `range`                | string   | Yes      | Cell range in A1 notation.                                     |
| `input_file`           | string   | Yes      | CSV file to upload.                                            |
| `value_input_option`   | string   | No       | `"USER_ENTERED"` (default) or `"RAW"`.                         |

## Key Function: `ExecuteCloud`

```cpp
bool ExecuteCloud(
    WorkflowDefinition const& workflowDefinition,
    WorkflowRun& workflowRun,
    TaskDef const& taskDefinition,
    TaskInstanceState& taskState,
    CloudConnection const& connection,
    CloudCredentials const& credentials,
    TaskCancellationToken const& cancellationToken
) override;
```

### Description

Processes a `sheets_read` or `sheets_write` cloud task by:

- Parsing task parameters (JSON).
- Resolving required information such as spreadsheet ID, range, input/output file.
- For `read`:
  - Issues a GET request to Sheets API for the specified range.
  - Writes output as CSV or JSON to the specified file.
  - Reports the number of rows read.
- For `write`:
  - Reads the input CSV file.
  - Constructs and sends the JSON payload with data to the Sheets API.
  - Reports the number of rows written.
- Handles API errors, sets task state and error messages accordingly.
- Stores a summary and API response for the workflow.

## Internal Utilities

Includes static helper functions for:

- JSON string escaping
- URL encoding for API calls
- CSV escape and parsing per RFC 4180
- Performing HTTP(S) API requests using libcurl
- Directory and file management (via C++17 `<filesystem>`)

## Authentication

- Supports both API key and OAuth2 (Bearer Token) auth modes.
- Handles token placement (query parameter or HTTP header) as appropriate.

## Error Handling

- Detects and reports API and file handling errors.
- Sets `taskState.m_State` to `Failed` on error, `Succeeded` on success.
- Captures a summary of the operation in `taskState.m_CapturedStdout`.
- Writes the raw Sheets API response to disk.

## Example Usage (Pseudocode)

```json
{
  "type": "sheets_read",
  "params": {
    "connection": "gdrive_conn",
    "spreadsheet_id": "<id>",
    "range": "Sheet1!A1:C10",
    "output_format": "csv"
  }
}
```

## License

GPL-3.0. See source for full license.

### 26_doc_application_cloud_googleSheetsConnector_h

<a id="26-doc-application-cloud-googlesheetsconnector-h"></a>

# GoogleSheetsConnector Class Documentation

**Namespace:** `AIAssistant`  
**Header:** `cloud/googleSheetsConnector.h`  
**Base Class:** `ICloudConnector`  
**API:** Google Sheets API v4

## Overview

The `GoogleSheetsConnector` class implements a cloud connector for accessing Google Sheets via the Google Sheets API v4.  
It supports both public (read-only, API key) and private (read/write, OAuth2) access methods.

## Usage

Provide a `CloudConnection` configured with:

- `m_Params["spreadsheet_id"]`: Google Sheets spreadsheet ID *(required)*
- `m_Endpoint`: Sheets API base URL *(optional, default: "https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets")*
- `m_KeyName`: KeyManager credential (API key or OAuth2 credential)
- `m_AuthType`: `BearerToken` (API key) or `OAuth2`

## Public Methods

### std::string GetType() const
Returns the connector type string: `"google_sheets"`.

---

### bool TestConnection(const CloudConnection& connection, std::string& errorMessage) override

Tests Google Sheets connectivity for the provided connection using the specified credentials and spreadsheet ID.

- Verifies the presence of `spreadsheet_id` in params.
- Resolves credentials (API Key or OAuth2 tokens).
- Sends a minimal GET request to verify the spreadsheet's accessibility.
- Returns `true` if connection is successful; `false` with `errorMessage` otherwise.

---

### bool ResolveCredentials(const CloudConnection& connection, CloudCredentials& credentials, std::string& errorMessage) override

Resolves credentials for authenticated access.

- If `AuthType` is `OAuth2`, attempts token retrieval using `OAuthTokenManager`.
- Falls back to API key lookup in `KeyManager` for public sheets (read-only).
- Sets `credentials.m_AuthType` and `credentials.m_Token`.
- Returns `true` if credentials found; otherwise `false` with error message.

---

### bool GetOAuth2ProviderInfo(const CloudConnection& connection, OAuth2ProviderInfo& info) const override

Fills `info` with Google OAuth2 endpoint URLs and required scopes:

- Authorize URL: `https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth`
- Token URL: `https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token`
- Default Scope: `https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets`
- Extra authorization params: `access_type=offline`, `prompt=consent`, `include_granted_scopes=true`
- Requires client secret: `true`

Always returns `true`.

---

### static std::string GetApiBaseUrl(const CloudConnection& connection)

Returns the API base URL from the connection (`connection.m_Endpoint`) or the default if not set.

---

## Constants

- `DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL`: `"https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets"`
- `DEFAULT_OAUTH_SCOPES`: `"https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets"`

## Auth Support

- **API Key** (`BearerToken`): For public, read-only Google Sheets access.
- **OAuth2**: For private or read/write Google Sheets access.

## Error Handling

Most methods return `false` and populate an error message (`errorMessage`) on failure.

## Dependencies

- `curl` for HTTP requests.
- `Core`, `KeyManager`, `OAuthTokenManager`, `CurlWrapper` for credentials and transport setup.

## Example

```cpp
AIAssistant::GoogleSheetsConnector connector;
CloudConnection conn;
// configure conn.m_Params["spreadsheet_id"], conn.m_KeyName, etc.
std::string error;
if (connector.TestConnection(conn, error)) {
    // Connection successful
} else {
    // Handle error
}
```

---

### 27_doc_application_cloud_jiraCloudTaskExecutor_h

<a id="27-doc-application-cloud-jiracloudtaskexecutor-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/27_doc_application_cloud_jiraCloudTaskExecutor_h/docu.output.md`



# JiraCloudTaskExecutor Class Documentation

**Namespace:** `AIAssistant`  
**Inherits from:** `ICloudTaskExecutor`  
**File:** `cloud/jiraCloudTaskExecutor.h`, `cloud/jiraCloudTaskExecutor.cpp`

## Overview

`JiraCloudTaskExecutor` is a task executor class for performing Jira issue operations via the Jira Cloud REST API v3. It is intended for use in workflow systems to automate issue management in Jira Cloud from code or workflow automation platforms.

---

## Supported Operations

The class supports the following Jira operations as specified by the `"operation"` field in the task params:

- `create`: Create a new Jira issue.
- `update`: Update fields of an existing Jira issue.
- `transition`: Transition a Jira issue using a workflow transition.
- `comment`: Add a comment to a Jira issue.
- `get`: Retrieve details of a Jira issue.

---

## Task JSON Parameter Keys (All keys are strings unless noted)

| Key              | Required?                   | Operation(s)         | Description                                            |
|------------------|----------------------------|----------------------|--------------------------------------------------------|
| `connection`     | Yes                        | all                  | Named CloudConnection for Jira Cloud API access        |
| `operation`      | Yes                        | all                  | Operation type: `create`, `update`, `transition`, `comment`, `get` |
| `project_key`    | Yes (`create`)*            | create               | Jira project key (can be optional if connection specifies a default) |
| `summary`        | Yes                        | create               | Jira issue summary                                     |
| `description`    | Optional                   | create               | Jira issue description                                 |
| `description_file` | Optional                 | create               | Path to a file for issue description (overrides inline description if set) |
| `issue_type`     | Optional (default: `Task`) | create               | Issue type name (Bug, Story, Task, etc.)               |
| `priority`       | Optional                   | create               | Jira issue priority name                               |
| `labels`         | Optional (JSON array)      | create               | Array of label strings                                 |
| `issue_key`      | Yes                        | update, transition, comment, get | Jira issue key (e.g. `PROJ-123`)              |
| `fields`         | Yes (JSON object)          | update               | Fields to update on the issue (raw JSON object)        |
| `transition_id`  | Yes                        | transition           | Transition ID to apply                                 |
| `body`           | Yes                        | comment              | Comment body text                                      |

\* `project_key` is required for `create` unless set as default in the connection.

---

## Core Method

### `bool ExecuteCloud(...)` (overrides `ICloudTaskExecutor::ExecuteCloud`)

Performs the requested Jira operation using the provided workflow/task/context parameters.

**On completion:**
- The response (typically JSON) is saved in `taskState.m_CapturedStdout`.
- Output is also saved to the task working directory as response JSON.
- Sets `taskState.m_State` to `Succeeded` or `Failed`. Logs error messages in `taskState.m_LastErrorMessage` on failure.

---

## Implementation Notes

- Uses the [Jira Cloud REST API v3](https://developer.atlassian.com/cloud/jira/platform/rest/v3/).
- Handles authentication via either BasicAuth (username/password) or Bearer token, as supplied in `CloudCredentials`.
- Uses libcurl for HTTP requests and simdjson for fast JSON parsing.
- Automatically escapes JSON fields to ensure proper request formatting.
- For `create` and `comment`, description and comment bodies are formatted in Atlassian Document Format (ADF), per Jira v3 API requirements.
- Truncates captured output to 1024 bytes by default for logging/state output.
- Allows reading issue description from a file for seamless AI or automation integration (`description_file`).

---

## Error Handling

- The function fails and records errors if mandatory parameters are missing or if HTTP requests fail (HTTP 4xx/5xx).
- Detailed error messages are set in `taskState.m_LastErrorMessage`.

---

## Example: Create Issue Task Param

```json
{
  "connection": "my_jira_cloud",
  "operation": "create",
  "project_key": "PROJ",
  "summary": "Sample bug",
  "description": "Created via API",
  "issue_type": "Bug",
  "priority": "High",
  "labels": ["api", "automation"]
}
```

## Example: Update Issue Task Param

```json
{
  "connection": "my_jira_cloud",
  "operation": "update",
  "issue_key": "PROJ-123",
  "fields": {
    "summary": "New summary",
    "priority": { "name": "Highest" }
  }
}
```

---

## See Also

- [Jira REST API v3 documentation](https://developer.atlassian.com/cloud/jira/platform/rest/v3/)
- Class `ICloudTaskExecutor`
- Class `CloudConnection` and `CloudCredentials`
- Structs: `WorkflowDefinition`, `WorkflowRun`, `TaskDef`, `TaskInstanceState`, `TaskCancellationToken`

---

## License

GPL-3.0 (see header comments for full license)


---

### 28_doc_application_cloud_jiraConnector_h

<a id="28-doc-application-cloud-jiraconnector-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/28_doc_application_cloud_jiraConnector_h/docu.output.md`



# JiraConnector C++ Class Documentation

## Overview

`JiraConnector` is a C++ class that implements a connector for Jira via the Jira REST API v3. It is part of the `AIAssistant` namespace and derives from `ICloudConnector`. This class enables integration with Jira Cloud or Jira Data Center to authenticate, test connectivity, and manage Jira instance credentials.

## Key Concepts

- Uses CloudConnection configuration to specify endpoint, credentials, and parameters.
- Supports Basic Authentication (email + API token) for Jira Cloud.
- Supports Bearer Token (Personal Access Token, PAT) for Jira Data Center.
- Utilizes libcurl for HTTP REST API requests.

## CloudConnection.m_Params Keys

- `project_key` — Default Jira project key (e.g., `"PROJ"`).

## CloudConnection Fields

- `m_Endpoint` — Jira instance URL (e.g., `"https://mycompany.atlassian.net"`).
- `m_KeyName` — Credential key (used to look up credentials in the KeyManager).
- `m_AuthType` — Specifies authentication type: `BasicAuth` or `BearerToken`.

---

## Public Member Functions

### std::string GetType() const

Returns the connector type identifier.  
**Return:** `"jira"`

### bool TestConnection(const CloudConnection& connection, std::string& errorMessage)

Tests the Jira connection by performing a GET request to `/rest/api/3/myself`.  
**Parameters:**
- `connection` — CloudConnection object with endpoint and credentials.
- `errorMessage` — Output parameter for error details.

**Returns** `true` if the connection succeeds and credentials are valid; `false` otherwise (with error message).

### bool ResolveCredentials(const CloudConnection& connection, CloudCredentials& credentials, std::string& errorMessage)

Resolves the credentials required for connecting to Jira.  
**Parameters:**
- `connection` — CloudConnection object referencing the key name.
- `credentials` — Output for resolved credentials (filled in on success).
- `errorMessage` — Output parameter for error details.

**Returns:** `true` if credentials are resolved successfully, `false` otherwise.

- Credentials can be:
  - BasicAuth (username/email + password/API token)
  - BearerToken (API/personal access token)

### static std::string GetBaseUrl(const CloudConnection& connection)

Returns the base URL for a Jira instance, omitting trailing slashes if present.  
**Parameter:**  
- `connection` — CloudConnection object with the endpoint.

**Return:** Base URL string.

---

## Usage Example

```cpp
AIAssistant::CloudConnection conn;
// Set conn fields (m_Endpoint, m_KeyName, etc.)

AIAssistant::JiraConnector jira;

std::string error;
if (jira.TestConnection(conn, error)) {
    // Connection to Jira succeeded
} else {
    // Handle connection error, see 'error'
}
```

---

## Dependencies

- Requires libcurl for HTTP operations.
- Depends on `cloud/cloudConnector.h`, `core.h`, `engine.h`, `keys/keyManager.h`, `curlWrapper/curlWrapper.h`.

---

## Error Handling

Errors are returned via the `errorMessage` output parameter for methods dealing with external resources or credential resolution.

---

GPL-3.0  
Copyright (c) 2026 JC Technolabs

### 29_doc_application_cloud_oneDriveCloudTaskExecutor_h

<a id="29-doc-application-cloud-onedrivecloudtaskexecutor-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/29_doc_application_cloud_oneDriveCloudTaskExecutor_h/docu.output.md`



# OneDriveCloudTaskExecutor

**Namespace:** `AIAssistant`  
**Header:** `cloud/oneDriveCloudTaskExecutor.h`  
**Inherits:** `ICloudTaskExecutor`

## Overview

`OneDriveCloudTaskExecutor` handles file upload and download tasks between the local file system and Microsoft OneDrive using the Microsoft Graph API. It integrates as a task type within a workflow system, enabling programmatic automation of OneDrive data transfers.

## Supported Task Types

- `onedrive_upload`
- `onedrive_download`

## Task Parameters (JSON keys)

All parameters are required.

| Key           | Description                                                         |
|---------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------|
| connection    | Named `CloudConnection` object for authentication and config         |
| remote_path   | Target file path on OneDrive, e.g. `"Documents/sample.txt"`         |
| local_path    | Local file path (relative to working directory), e.g. `"out/file"`  |
| operation     | String: `"upload"` or `"download"`                                 |

## Main Method

### `bool ExecuteCloud(...) override`

Executes the OneDrive upload or download operation as specified in the task parameters. Handles authentication, parameter parsing, local/remote file I/O, and error management.

#### Arguments

- `WorkflowDefinition const& workflowDefinition`  
  Workflow root for resolving paths.
- `WorkflowRun& workflowRun`  
  State of workflow execution.
- `TaskDef const& taskDefinition`  
  Task metadata, including JSON parameters and working directory.
- `TaskInstanceState& taskState`  
  Updated with status, error messages, responses.
- `CloudConnection const& connection`  
  Contains configuration details for Graph API access.
- `CloudCredentials const& credentials`  
  Holds authentication token for API access.
- `TaskCancellationToken const& cancellationToken`  
  For interrupting/cancelling operation (not actively checked in code).

#### Returns

- `true` if operation succeeded.
- `false` if operation failed. Updates `taskState` with error info.

## Behavior

- Parses task parameters from JSON (validates required keys).
- Resolves local file path relative to task's working directory.
- For `"upload"`:
  - Reads the local file into memory.
  - Sends HTTP PUT request to OneDrive using Graph API to upload file.
- For `"download"`:
  - Ensures the target directory exists.
  - Sends HTTP GET request to OneDrive using Graph API to download file.
  - Saves content to local file.
- Captures partial API response (up to 1024 characters) in `taskState`.
- Marks `taskState.m_State` as `Succeeded` or `Failed`.
- Writes response JSON to task's working directory.

## Error Handling

- Fails if any required parameter is missing.
- Reports network, file, or HTTP errors in `taskState.m_LastErrorMessage`.
- HTTP status codes >= 400 are treated as errors.

## Helper Functions

Internal static functions:

- `GraphRequest` — Performs authenticated Graph API requests with cURL.
- `GraphDownload` — Downloads large files efficiently to disk using cURL.

## Security & Limits

- Uses Bearer token authentication.
- Upload and download operations are capped by hardcoded timeout (5 min) and max file size (256 MB for downloads).
- Uses OpenSSL CA bundle for HTTPS, if configured.

## Example (Task JSON)

```json
{
  "operation": "upload",
  "connection": "onedrive-account-1",
  "remote_path": "Documents/backup.dat",
  "local_path": "local/backup.dat"
}
```

or

```json
{
  "operation": "download",
  "connection": "onedrive-account-1",
  "remote_path": "Reports/summary.pdf",
  "local_path": "downloads/summary.pdf"
}
```

## Logging

- Logs upload or download actions with source and destination paths.

## Dependencies

- `cURL` for HTTP requests.
- `simdjson` for JSON parsing.
- `filesystem` for path operations.

## License

GPL-3.0

---

**Summary:**  
`OneDriveCloudTaskExecutor` efficiently automates OneDrive file upload and download tasks in a workflow environment, with strong error handling and minimal configuration required per task.


---

### 30_doc_application_cloud_oneDriveConnector_h

<a id="30-doc-application-cloud-onedriveconnector-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/30_doc_application_cloud_oneDriveConnector_h/docu.output.md`



# OneDriveConnector Class Documentation

## Overview

`OneDriveConnector` is a C++ class implementing the `ICloudConnector` interface. It provides Microsoft OneDrive connectivity through the Microsoft Graph API, using OAuth2 authentication via Azure AD.

Defined in: `cloud/oneDriveConnector.h`  
Namespace: `AIAssistant`

## Purpose

Facilitates interaction with Microsoft OneDrive cloud storage. Manages OAuth2 authentication (token resolution), builds API endpoint URLs, and verifies connection credentials.

---

## Connection Configuration

- **CloudConnection.m_Params keys:**
  - `client_id` (required): Azure AD application (client) ID.
  - `tenant_id` (optional): Azure AD tenant ID. Default: `"common"` (multi-tenant).
  - `scopes` (optional): OAuth scopes. Default: `"Files.ReadWrite offline_access"`.

- **CloudConnection members:**
  - `m_Endpoint`: Graph API base URL. Default: `https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0`.
  - `m_KeyName`: KeyManager credential (must be OAuthCredential managed by OAuthTokenManager).
  - `m_AuthType`: Must be `OAuth2`.

---

## Public Methods

### std::string GetType() const

Returns the connector type string:  
- `"onedrive"`

---

### bool TestConnection(const CloudConnection& connection, std::string& errorMessage) override

Tests the connection to OneDrive by:
- Validates presence of `client_id`.
- Resolves OAuth credentials.
- Sends a GET `/me/drive` request using the access token.
- Sets `errorMessage` on failure.
- Returns `true` if access is successful; `false` otherwise.

---

### bool ResolveCredentials(const CloudConnection& connection, CloudCredentials& credentials, std::string& errorMessage) override

Resolves and loads OAuth2 credentials by:
- Checking `m_KeyName` is set.
- Fetching access token from `OAuthTokenManager`.
- Populating `credentials.m_AuthType` and `credentials.m_Token`.
- Sets `errorMessage` on failure.
- Returns `true` on success; `false` otherwise.

---

### bool GetOAuth2ProviderInfo(const CloudConnection& connection, OAuth2ProviderInfo& info) const override

Fills `info` with:
- `m_AuthorizeUrl` and `m_TokenUrl` (based on `tenant_id` or default).
- Default scopes.
- PKCE (no client secret needed).
- Extra authorize params (`response_mode=query`).
- Returns `true`.

---

## Static Methods

### std::string GetGraphBaseUrl(const CloudConnection& connection)

Returns the OneDrive Graph API base URL:
- Uses `m_Endpoint` if set (trailing slash trimmed), otherwise default (`https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0`).

---

### std::string GetAuthorizeUrl(const std::string& tenantId)

Returns the OAuth2 authorize endpoint for the specified or default tenant.

---

### std::string GetTokenUrl(const std::string& tenantId)

Returns the OAuth2 token endpoint for the specified or default tenant.

---

## Constants

- `DEFAULT_GRAPH_BASE_URL`: `"https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0"`
- `DEFAULT_SCOPES`: `"Files.ReadWrite offline_access"`
- `DEFAULT_TENANT_ID`: `"common"`

---

## Usage Notes

- Requires the `client_id` parameter and an OAuth credential managed by `OAuthTokenManager`.
- `TestConnection` performs a real OneDrive API request to verify authentication and connection.
- Supports PKCE public client flow (no client secret needed).

---

## Example

```
CloudConnection conn;
conn.m_KeyName = "my-onedrive";
conn.m_Params["client_id"] = "<azure-client-id>";
OneDriveConnector connector;
std::string error;
if (!connector.TestConnection(conn, error)) {
    // error contains failure reason
}
```


---

### 31_doc_application_cloud_polarionConnector_h

<a id="31-doc-application-cloud-polarionconnector-h"></a>

# PolarionConnector Class Documentation

**Namespace:** `AIAssistant`  
**Header:** `cloud/polarionConnector.h`  
**Base Class:** `ICloudConnector`  
**License:** GPL-3.0

## Overview

`PolarionConnector` provides a connector to Polarion ALM, wrapping an existing `PolarionClient` for use with a cloud abstraction. It enables integration, credential resolution, and connectivity verification using Polarion's REST API.

## Configuration

- **CloudConnection.m_Endpoint:** Base URL of the Polarion server (e.g., `https://polarion.example.com`).
- **CloudConnection.m_Params["project_id"]:** Required. The Polarion project ID.
- **CloudConnection.m_KeyName:** Required. KeyManager credential holding the PAT (`api_key` type).

## Public Methods

### std::string GetType() const

Returns the connector type identifier string.

- **Return:** `"polarion"`

### bool TestConnection(const CloudConnection& connection, std::string& errorMessage)

Tests connectivity to the specified Polarion instance.

- **Parameters:**
  - `connection`: Cloud connection configuration.
  - `errorMessage`: Reference for error messages.
- **Behavior:**
  - Ensures endpoint and `project_id` parameter are present.
  - Resolves credentials via `ResolveCredentials`.
  - Executes a minimal REST API query to verify access.
- **Returns:** `true` if connection and credentials are valid, `false` otherwise.

### bool ResolveCredentials(const CloudConnection& connection, CloudCredentials& credentials, std::string& errorMessage)

Resolves API credentials for Polarion from the KeyManager.

- **Parameters:**
  - `connection`: Cloud connection configuration.
  - `credentials`: Populated if resolution succeeds.
  - `errorMessage`: Reference for error messages.
- **Behavior:**
  - Ensures `m_KeyName` is provided.
  - Looks up the credential in KeyManager.
  - Sets `credentials.m_Token` to the API key, marks auth type as BearerToken.
- **Returns:** `true` if credentials are found and valid, `false` otherwise.

## Usage Example

```cpp
AIAssistant::PolarionConnector connector;
CloudConnection conn;
conn.m_Endpoint = "https://polarion.example.com";
conn.m_Params["project_id"] = "your_project_id";
conn.m_KeyName = "your_key_name";
std::string error;
if (!connector.TestConnection(conn, error)) {
    // Handle error
}
```

## Error Reporting

On failure, `TestConnection` and `ResolveCredentials` set `errorMessage` with a concise description of the issue.

## See Also

- `ICloudConnector`
- `PolarionClient`
- `KeyManager`
- `CloudConnection`
- `CloudCredentials`


---

### 32_doc_application_cloud_polarionWriteTaskExecutor_h

<a id="32-doc-application-cloud-polarionwritetaskexecutor-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/32_doc_application_cloud_polarionWriteTaskExecutor_h/docu.output.md`



# PolarionWriteTaskExecutor Class Documentation

## Overview

`PolarionWriteTaskExecutor` is a task executor class for performing write operations in Polarion ALM. It supports update, create, attachment (upload/download), and linked item retrieval for Polarion work items.

**Namespace:** `AIAssistant`  
**Base class:** `ICloudTaskExecutor`  
**Source:** `cloud/polarionWriteTaskExecutor.h`, `cloud/polarionWriteTaskExecutor.cpp`

---

## Purpose

Executes tasks of JCWF type `"polarion_write"` as part of a workflow, allowing automation of common Polarion ALM work item operations via cloud connections.

---

## Supported Operations

Specify the `"operation"` key in task params:

- `"update"`: Update fields on existing work items.
- `"create"`: Create new work items.
- `"upload_attachment"`: Upload file attachments to a work item.
- `"download_attachment"`: Download an attachment from a work item.
- `"linked_items"`: Retrieve the set of linked items for a work item.

---

## Task Parameters

**All operations require suitable task parameters as JSON keys:**

| Key                | Type    | Required | For Operation(s)      | Description                                 |
|--------------------|---------|----------|-----------------------|---------------------------------------------|
| connection         | string  | Yes      | all                   | Name of the predefined CloudConnection      |
| operation          | string  | Yes      | all                   | One of the above supported operations       |
| work_item_id       | string  | Yes      | update/upload_attachment/download_attachment/linked_items | Target work item ID          |
| attachment_id      | string  | Yes      | download_attachment   | ID of the attachment to download            |
| file_path          | string  | Yes      | upload/download_attachment | Local file path to upload/download       |
| file_name          | string  | No       | upload_attachment     | Override attachment filename (otherwise the local filename is used)    |
| body               | string  | See note | update/create         | JSON:API request body to send (required unless field_name is used)     |
| field_name         | string  | No       | update                | Name of single field to update (builds request automatically)             |
| field_value        | string  | No       | update                | Value for field_name (plain text, escapes JSON internally)              |
| field_value_file   | string  | No       | update                | Path to file whose contents become field value (overrides field_value)   |

**Notes:**
- `body` is required for `create`. For `update`, either `body` or a combination of `field_name` + `field_value`/`field_value_file` must be provided.
- For `upload_attachment`, if `file_name` is not specified, the basename of `file_path` is used.

---

## Outputs

| Key       | Description                                 |
|-----------|---------------------------------------------|
| response  | Raw JSON:API response body from Polarion.   |

- The value is written to task output as both captured stdout (truncated to 1024 chars) and to a `response.json` in the task's working directory.

---

## Error Handling

- Missing or invalid parameters result in immediate failure with a clear error message in `taskState.m_LastErrorMessage`.
- Unsupported operations fail with an explicit error.
- Most errors reported from the underlying Polarion API are captured and reported.

---

## Implementation Details

- Uses `simdjson` for fast JSON parsing of task parameters.
- Field-value update operations build escaped JSON:API bodies automatically.
- File I/O is used for attachment operations and for reading field values from files.
- Responses are capped at 1024 characters for `capturedStdout` but fully written to the output file.

---

## Example Task Param JSON

Update work item:
```json
{
  "connection": "my_polarion",
  "operation": "update",
  "work_item_id": "WI-12345",
  "field_name": "description",
  "field_value": "Updated via automation"
}
```

Create a new work item:
```json
{
  "connection": "my_polarion",
  "operation": "create",
  "body": "{...}"  // Proper JSON:API request body
}
```

Upload an attachment:
```json
{
  "connection": "my_polarion",
  "operation": "upload_attachment",
  "work_item_id": "WI-12345",
  "file_path": "/tmp/file.docx"
}
```

---

## Inheritance

Overrides:
```cpp
bool ExecuteCloud(WorkflowDefinition const&, WorkflowRun&, TaskDef const&, TaskInstanceState&, CloudConnection const&, CloudCredentials const&, TaskCancellationToken const&) override;
```

---

## See Also

- `PolarionClient` (for actual Polarion API interaction)
- `ICloudTaskExecutor`
- Task param specs in JCWF documentation

---


---

### 33_doc_application_cloud_postgresConnector_h

<a id="33-doc-application-cloud-postgresconnector-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/33_doc_application_cloud_postgresConnector_h/docu.output.md`



# PostgresConnector Class Documentation

**Namespace:** `AIAssistant`  
**Header:** `cloud/postgresConnector.h`  
**Implements:** `ICloudConnector`

PostgresConnector provides a connector for PostgreSQL databases using the libpq C API.

## Purpose

Facilitates connecting, testing, and credential resolution for PostgreSQL databases in a cloud environment by constructing libpq-compliant connection strings and validating connectivity.

## Member Functions

### std::string GetType() const

- **Returns:**  
  String `"postgres"`.
- **Purpose:**  
  Identifies the connector type.

---

### bool ResolveCredentials(const CloudConnection& connection, CloudCredentials& credentials, std::string& errorMessage)

- **Parameters:**  
  - `connection`: The cloud connection configuration.
  - `credentials`: Output parameter. On success, filled with resolved username and password.
  - `errorMessage`: Output parameter. Receives an error message if resolution fails.
- **Returns:**  
  - `true` if credentials are resolved.
  - `false` if credentials are missing or invalid; `errorMessage` populated.
- **Behavior:**  
  - Retrieves credential information using the `KeyManager` based on `m_KeyName` in `CloudConnection`.
  - Enforces presence of both credential key and username.

---

### static std::string BuildConnectionString(const CloudConnection& connection, const CloudCredentials& credentials)

- **Parameters:**  
  - `connection`: Connection configuration (host, port, database, SSL mode, etc.).
  - `credentials`: Resolved authentication details.
- **Returns:**  
  A valid [libpq connection string](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/libpq-connect.html#LIBPQ-CONNSTRING) composed using input data.
- **Behavior:**  
  - Parses `m_Endpoint` as `host[:port]`, defaulting to `localhost:5432`.
  - Extracts database name (`database`) and SSL mode (`sslmode`) from `m_Params`.
  - Escapes values as single-quoted strings for libpq compatibility.
  - Always sets a 10-second connection timeout.

---

### bool TestConnection(const CloudConnection& connection, std::string& errorMessage)

- **Parameters:**  
  - `connection`: The cloud connection configuration.
  - `errorMessage`: Output parameter. Set if connection fails.
- **Returns:**  
  - `true` if the PostgreSQL connection and test query succeed.
  - `false` if connection or test query fails; `errorMessage` populated.
- **Behavior:**  
  - Verifies required `database` parameter.
  - Resolves credentials using `ResolveCredentials`.
  - Builds the libpq connection string.
  - Attempts connection with `PQconnectdb`.
  - Executes a test query (`SELECT 1`). Both connection and query must succeed.

## Configuration Details

- **`CloudConnection.m_Endpoint`:**  
  Hostname or `host:port` (e.g., `"myhost:5432"`). If port missing, defaults to `5432`.
- **`CloudConnection.m_KeyName`:**  
  Name of credential in the `KeyManager` (BasicAuth required).
- **`CloudConnection.m_Params`:**
  - `database` (required): Database name.
  - `sslmode` (optional): SSL mode (`disable`, `require`, `verify-ca`, `verify-full`, default `prefer`).

## Error Handling

Errors are reported by populating the `errorMessage` output parameter and returning `false`.

## Example Usage

```cpp
CloudConnection conn;
conn.m_Endpoint = "myhost:5432";
conn.m_KeyName = "my_pg_creds";
conn.m_Params["database"] = "mydb";
conn.m_Params["sslmode"] = "require";

AIAssistant::PostgresConnector pg;
std::string error;

if (pg.TestConnection(conn, error)) {
    // Connection successful
} else {
    // Handle error
    std::cerr << "Error: " << error << std::endl;
}
```

## License

GPL-3.0  
See source code for license details.
