### 51_doc_application_json_jcwfSchema_generated_h

<a id="51-doc-application-json-jcwfschema-generated-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/51_doc_application_json_jcwfSchema_generated_h/docu.output.md`



# Documentation: `AIAssistant::kJcwfSchemaJson`

## Overview

`AIAssistant::kJcwfSchemaJson` is an inline, constant C++ string containing the JSON schema for "JC Workflow File (JCWF) v1.1". This schema defines the structure and validation rules for JCWF files, which are used to describe automated workflows involving tasks, triggers, dataflows, and more.

**Note:**  
- This header is auto-generated. Do not edit by hand.
- License: GPL-3.0  
- Source: `doc/jcwf.schema.json`

---

## Purpose

- Defines the data schema for JCWF files in JSON Schema Draft 2020-12.
- Ensures proper validation and structure of workflow configuration files for JC Tech workflows.
- Used by both humans and AIs to read, validate, or generate workflow configurations.

---

## Schema Structure

### Top-Level Properties

| Property          | Type        | Description                                         | Required  |
|-------------------|-------------|-----------------------------------------------------|-----------|
| `version`         | string      | JCWF schema version (pattern: `^1\.[01]$`)          | Yes       |
| `id`              | string      | Unique workflow identifier                          | Yes       |
| `label`           | string      | Optional workflow label                             | No        |
| `doc`             | string/array| Documentation as a string or array of strings       | No        |
| `base_directory`  | string      | Base directory for workflow files                   | No        |
| `manual_start`    | boolean     | Whether workflow requires manual start (default: true) | No    |
| `triggers`        | array       | Workflow trigger definitions                        | No        |
| `tasks`           | object      | Workflow tasks definition (ID to task config)       | Yes       |
| `dataflow`        | array       | Data transfer between tasks                         | No        |
| `filters`         | array       | Data input/output filters                           | No        |
| `control_nodes`   | array       | Control nodes for logical workflow flow (e.g., branch) | No     |
| `controlflow`     | array       | Edges between control nodes/tasks                   | No        |
| `defaults`        | object      | Default configurations (timeouts, retries, AI, etc) | No        |

---

### Key Definitions

#### `trigger`
Defines automatic or manual workflow triggers.  
Fields: `type`, `id`, `enabled`, `params`.  
Supported types: `auto`, `cron`, `file_watch`, `manual`, `webhook`, `s3_watch`, etc.

#### `task`
Defines executable units in the workflow.  
Fields: `id`, `type` (e.g., `python`, `shell`, `ai_call`, etc.), `depends_on`, file inputs/outputs, environment settings, parameters, retries, outputs, and more.

#### `queue_file_ref`
References to files, either by string path or as an object containing `path` and `content`.

#### `dataflow`
Describes how data moves between tasks.  
Fields: `from_task`, `from_output`, `to_task`, `to_input`, etc.

#### `filter`
Describes transformations and input-output constraints for data sources.  
Supported sources: `csv`, `text_lines`, `query`, `polarion_query`.

#### `control_node` / `controlflow_edge`
Specifies logical/conditional nodes and the connections in the workflow's execution flow graph.

---

## Usage

- Applications can include this header to access the JCWF schema string and use it for runtime validation or documentation of JCWF files.
- When defining or consuming JCWF files, ensure objects match types/required fields and enumerated values as specified in the schema.

---

## Limitations

- The class is purely a string constant; it does not provide runtime schema parsing or validation logic. That must be implemented by the caller (e.g., using a JSON Schema library).
- Auto-generated; changes should be made in the schema source, not in this header.

---

## Example

To validate a JCWF file or generate one:

1. Load the schema from `AIAssistant::kJcwfSchemaJson`.
2. Use a JSON Schema validator for your language/library to check JCWF file compliance.
3. Use property/definition documentation above to construct valid JCWF config files for automation.

---

## License

GPL-3.0 (c) 2026 JC Technolabs

---

## Reference

- [JSON Schema Draft 2020-12](https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema)

---

**For further details, see the schema source in `doc/jcwf.schema.json`.**


---

### 52_doc_application_json_jsonObjectParser_h

<a id="52-doc-application-json-jsonobjectparser-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/52_doc_application_json_jsonObjectParser_h/docu.output.md`



# AIAssistant::JsonObjectParser Documentation

## Overview

`AIAssistant::JsonObjectParser` is a utility class for parsing and logging JSON objects using the [simdjson](https://github.com/simdjson/simdjson) library. It provides recursive traversal of JSON data, pretty-printing, and structured error handling. The class is designed for debugging, inspection, or transformation of JSON values in C++ applications.

---

## Class Definition

```cpp
class JsonObjectParser
{
public:
    JsonObjectParser(std::string_view key, simdjson::ondemand::value value, std::string const& warningText, uint32_t indentLevel = 0);
    ~JsonObjectParser() = default;

    bool HasError() const;

private:
    void Parse();
    void ParseObject(simdjson::ondemand::object obj);
    void ParseArray(simdjson::ondemand::array arr);

    std::string PrintIndented() const;

private:
    bool m_HasError{false};
    std::string m_Key;
    simdjson::ondemand::value m_Value;
    std::string m_WarningText;
    uint32_t m_IndentLevel{0};
};
```

---

## Constructor

### `JsonObjectParser(std::string_view key, simdjson::ondemand::value value, const std::string& warningText, uint32_t indentLevel = 0)`

- **key**: The JSON object's key or an array index label.
- **value**: The JSON value to parse (simdjson view).
- **warningText**: Text to include with warning logs for context.
- **indentLevel**: Controls pretty-print indentation for nested structures (default: 0).

**Action:** Immediately parses the supplied JSON value, logging its contents recursively.

---

## Methods

### `bool HasError() const`

- **Returns**: `true` if an error occurred during parsing; `false` otherwise.

Checks if the parser encountered any error during traversal of the JSON value.

### `void Parse()`

- **Access**: private (called implicitly by the constructor)
- **Purpose**: Detects the type of the JSON value and dispatches to the appropriate parsing function.
- **Behavior**:
    - Handles `string`, `number`, `boolean`, `null`, `object`, and `array` types.
    - Logs each field/value with contextual indentation.
    - On error, logs a warning and sets the error flag.

### `void ParseObject(simdjson::ondemand::object obj)`

- **Access**: private
- **Purpose**: Recursively parses each field of a JSON object.
- **Behavior**: 
    - For each field, creates a new `JsonObjectParser` with incremented indentation.
    - Logs and continues on malformed fields.

### `void ParseArray(simdjson::ondemand::array arr)`

- **Access**: private
- **Purpose**: Recursively parses each element of a JSON array.
- **Behavior**:
    - For each element, creates a new `JsonObjectParser` with incremented indentation and the current index as key.
    - Logs and continues on malformed elements.

### `std::string PrintIndented() const`

- **Access**: private
- **Purpose**: Returns an indentation string (spaces for indentation) based on the current level.
- **Used by**: Internal logging to format output.

---

## Usage

Intended for stepwise parsing and logging/debugging of JSON data structures. Create an instance of `JsonObjectParser` with the root key/value to begin logging the entire hierarchy.

**Example Usage:**
```cpp
simdjson::ondemand::parser parser;
auto doc = parser.iterate(json_string);
AIAssistant::JsonObjectParser("root", doc, "JSONParseWarning");
```

---

## Error Handling

- Uses simdjson exception handling for parsing errors.
- If parsing a value or structure fails, logs the error and sets `m_HasError = true`.
- `HasError()` can be checked after construction to determine if a problem occurred.

---

## Logging

- Relies on `LOG_APP_INFO` and `LOG_APP_WARN` macros (assumed to be application-defined) for output.
- Indents logs based on the nesting level.
- Reports unknown types and warns of parsing failures, including key and context.

---

## Dependencies

- Requires [simdjson](https://github.com/simdjson/simdjson) for fast JSON parsing.
- Relies on logging macros (`LOG_APP_INFO`, `LOG_APP_WARN`).

---

## Limitations

- The class is designed for inspection/logging; it does not offer API for programmatic tree access.
- The `simdjson::ondemand::value` must remain valid for the parser's lifetime (depends on simdjson's buffer handling).

---

## Summary

`AIAssistant::JsonObjectParser` is a recursive, logging-oriented JSON parser leveraging simdjson for efficient JSON structure traversal. It is well-suited for diagnostic and trace output during development, providing structured, indented logs and detailed error messages.


---

### 53_doc_application_json_replyParser_h

<a id="53-doc-application-json-replyparser-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/53_doc_application_json_replyParser_h/docu.output.md`



# AIAssistant::ReplyParser

The `AIAssistant::ReplyParser` is an abstract base class for parsing JSON replies from different AI service providers. It defines unified interfaces for parsing and accessing reply data, managing errors, and obtaining metadata, regardless of the provider-specific reply format.

## Overview

- Supports multiple AI provider APIs via derived classes (e.g., API1-API6).
- Provides provider-agnostic access to reply content, errors, usage, and metadata.
- Factory method constructs the appropriate parser based on the provider interface type.

## Class Definition

```cpp
class ReplyParser
```

### State Enum

Defines the internal state of the parser:
- `Undefined`
- `ParseOk`
- `ParseFailure`
- `ReplyOk`
- `ReplyError`

### Constructor

- `ReplyParser(const std::string& jsonString)`
  - Initializes with the raw JSON reply string.

### Destructor

- `virtual ~ReplyParser() = default`
  - Virtual destructor for safe inheritance.

## Core Methods

### Error and State

- `bool HasError() const`
  - Returns `true` if parsing failed or reply indicates an error.
- `AiError GetError() const`
  - Returns an `AiError` object when a parsing or provider error is detected.

### Content Access

- `virtual size_t HasContent() const = 0`
  - Returns number of reply contents; must be overridden.
- `virtual std::string GetContent(size_t index = 0) const = 0`
  - Returns the text/content at the given index; must be overridden.

### Metadata & Usage

- `virtual AiUsage GetUsage() const`
  - Returns usage data if available. Returns default otherwise.
- `virtual std::string GetFinishReason() const`
  - Returns provider-reported finish reason, if any.
- `virtual std::string GetSystemFingerprint() const`
  - Returns a system-specific fingerprint string, if available.
- `virtual std::optional<std::string> GetStructuredOutput() const`
  - Returns structured output if provided, else `std::nullopt`.

## Factory

- `static std::unique_ptr<ReplyParser> Create(const ConfigParser::EngineConfig::InterfaceType& interfaceType, const std::string& jsonString)`
  - Creates and returns a provider-specific parser instance based on `interfaceType`. Supports API1–API6. API6 reuses API1 parser for compatible responses. Returns `nullptr` if not supported.

## Internal Data Members

- `ReplyParser::State m_State`
  - Holds the current parser state.
- `std::string m_JsonString`
  - Stores the original JSON reply.
- `bool m_HasError`
  - Indicates if an error was detected.

## Extending

To support a new AI provider:
1. Implement a derived class for the provider and override pure virtual methods.
2. Add the new interface type to the `Create` method's switch statement.

## Dependencies

- `<memory>`, `<optional>`, `<string>`
- `"json/configParser.h"`
- `"workflow/aiReply.h"`

## Example Usage

```cpp
auto parser = ReplyParser::Create(interfaceType, jsonString);
if (parser && !parser->HasError()) {
    size_t count = parser->HasContent();
    if (count > 0) {
        std::string replyText = parser->GetContent();
        // process replyText
    }
}
```

## Notes

- This class does not implement any guessing or heuristics; it acts based on concrete JSON responses and configuration.
- Derived classes for each supported API handle the provider-specific parsing.

## License

MIT License — see source file headers for details.


---

### 54_doc_application_json_replyParserAPI1_h

<a id="54-doc-application-json-replyparserapi1-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/54_doc_application_json_replyParserAPI1_h/docu.output.md`



# ReplyParserAPI1 Documentation

**Namespace:** `AIAssistant`  
**Inherits:** `ReplyParser`

## Purpose

`ReplyParserAPI1` parses and extracts information from responses returned by GPT-4-style JSON APIs. It handles both successful replies and error messages, providing structured access to content, status, usage, and errors.

## Key Features

- Parses and extracts choices/messages from GPT-like chat completions.
- Handles error responses, providing detailed error info and mapping error types.
- Extracts token usage statistics (prompt, completion, total).
- Designed for robust and efficient parsing with simdjson.

---

## Types

### Structs

#### Reply
Contains full API response details.

- **m_Id:** string, unique request ID.
- **m_Object:** string, object type.
- **m_Created:** int, timestamp.
- **m_Model:** string, model name.
- **m_Choices:** vector of `Choice`, chat response options.
- **m_Usage:** `Usage`, token usage stats.

##### Choice
- **m_Index:** uint64, index of this choice.
- **m_Message:** `Message`, chat message info.
- **m_FinishReason:** string, why generation stopped.

###### Message
- **m_Role:** string, role (`assistant`, etc).
- **m_Content:** string, response text.

##### Usage
- **m_PromptTokens:** uint64, # of prompt tokens.
- **m_CompletionTokens:** uint64, # of completion tokens.
- **m_TotalTokens:** uint64, sum tokens.

#### ErrorInfo
Returns details for error replies.
- **m_Message:** string, error message.
- **m_Type:** string, error type.
- **m_Code:** string, error code.
- **m_Param:** string, error parameter (if provided).

### Enums

#### ErrorType

- **Unknown**
- **InvalidRequestError**
- **AuthenticationError**
- **PermissionError**
- **RateLimitError**
- **ServerError**
- **InsufficientQuota**

---

## Constructors and Destructor

- **ReplyParserAPI1(std::string const& jsonString);**  
  Constructs from a JSON reply, triggers parsing.

- **~ReplyParserAPI1();**  
  Virtual destructor (default).

---

## Public Methods

### Error & Usage

- **ErrorInfo const& GetErrorInfo() const;**  
  Returns parsed error information (if any).

- **ErrorType GetErrorType() const;**  
  Type of error detected, or `Unknown`.

- **AiError GetError() const override;**  
  Returns an error object for high-level handling (status, message).

- **AiUsage GetUsage() const override;**  
  Returns token usage stats.

### Content Access

- **size_t HasContent() const override;**  
  Returns the number of response choices/messages.

- **std::string GetContent(size_t index = 0) const override;**  
  Returns assistant message content for given choice index.

- **std::string GetFinishReason() const override;**  
  Returns `"finish_reason"` string, if present.

- **std::string GetSystemFingerprint() const override;**  
  Always returns empty string (no implementation).

---

## Private Methods

Handles detailed parsing, only for internal use.

- **void Parse();**  
  Main entry, controls dispatch to other parsing helpers.

- **void ParseContent(simdjson::ondemand::array, Reply&);**  
  Extracts choices and their messages from JSON.

- **void ParseUsage(simdjson::ondemand::object);**  
  Extracts token usage stats.

- **void ParseError(simdjson::ondemand::object);**  
  Extracts details for error replies.

- **ErrorType ParseErrorType(std::string_view type);**  
  Maps error type strings to `ErrorType` enum.

---

## Typical Workflow

1. Construct with JSON reply string.
2. Use `HasContent` and `GetContent` to access assistant replies.
3. Use `GetUsage` for token info.
4. Use `GetErrorInfo` and `GetErrorType` if `HasContent()` == 0 or an error occurred.

---

## Example JSON Handled

```json
{
  "id": "chatcmpl-abc123",
  "object": "chat.completion",
  "created": 1677858242,
  "model": "gpt-4.1",
  "choices": [
    {
      "index": 0,
      "message": {
        "role": "assistant",
        "content": "The engine meets the specified power output requirements."
      },
      "finish_reason": "stop"
    }
  ],
  "usage": {
    "prompt_tokens": 55,
    "completion_tokens": 17,
    "total_tokens": 72
  }
}
```

---

## Error Example

```json
{
  "error": {
    "message": "Invalid authentication.",
    "type": "authentication_error",
    "code": "invalid_api_key",
    "param": null
  }
}
```

---

## Notes

- Uses simdjson for fast JSON parsing.
- Handles unknown fields via fallback parser.
- Logs extensively during parsing.
- Designed for robust use in both human- and AI-interfaced applications.


---

### 55_doc_application_json_replyParserAPI2_h

# AIAssistant::ReplyParserAPI2 Documentation

## Overview

`ReplyParserAPI2` is a C++ class for parsing and extracting information from AI model responses in GPT-5-like JSON format. It efficiently decodes outputs, errors, and usage statistics from AI service replies.

- Inherits from: `ReplyParser`
- Dependencies: [simdjson](https://github.com/simdjson/simdjson)

## Main Features

- Parses structured AI responses in JSON format.
- Extracts assistant content, error info, error types, and token usage.
- Handles multiple response types, including message and reasoning.
- Provides programmatic access to extracted data.

## Example Input

A typical parsed JSON would look like:

```json
{
  "id": "resp_abcd...",
  "object": "response",
  "created_at": 1762230881,
  "status": "completed",
  "model": "gpt-5-nano-2025-08-07",
  "output": [
    {
      "id": "rs_efgh...",
      "type": "reasoning",
      "summary": []
    },
    {
      "id": "msg_ijkl...",
      "type": "message",
      "status": "completed",
      "content": [
        { "type": "output_text", "annotations": [], "logprobs": [], "text": "AI reply here." }
      ],
      "role": "assistant"
    }
  ],
  "usage": {
    "input_tokens": 14,
    "output_tokens": 726,
    "total_tokens": 740
  }
}
```

## Class Members

### Data Structures

#### `struct Reply`
- **Output**: List of outputs (messages/reasoning).
  - `id` (string)
  - `type` ("message" / "reasoning")
  - `status` (string)
  - `role` ("assistant" / "user")
  - `content`: vector of `Content`
    - Each `Content` contains:
      - `type` (e.g., "output_text")
      - `text` (string)
- **Usage**:
  - `input_tokens` (uint64)
  - `output_tokens` (uint64)
  - `total_tokens` (uint64)
- Top-level:
  - `id` (string)
  - `object` (string)
  - `created_at` (uint64)
  - `status` (string)
  - `model` (string)
  - `output` (vector of Output)
  - `usage` (Usage struct)

#### `struct ErrorInfo`
- `message` (string)
- `type` (string)
- `code` (string)
- `param` (string)

#### `enum class ErrorType`
- `Unknown`
- `InvalidRequestError`
- `AuthenticationError`
- `PermissionError`
- `RateLimitError`
- `ServerError`
- `InsufficientQuota`

### Constructor & Destructor

- **`ReplyParserAPI2(const std::string& jsonString)`**  
  Parses and stores the JSON reply.

- **`virtual ~ReplyParserAPI2() = default`**  
  Default destructor.

### Main Methods

- **`ErrorInfo const& GetErrorInfo() const`**  
  Returns details of any encountered error.

- **`ErrorType GetErrorType() const`**  
  Returns the enumerated error type.

- **`size_t HasContent() const`**  
  Returns the number of outputs containing text content.

- **`std::string GetContent(size_t index = 0) const`**  
  Returns the text of the output at the specified index. Returns empty string if index out of range or no content.

- **`AiError GetError() const`**  
  Returns structured provider error, if any.

- **`AiUsage GetUsage() const`**  
  Returns usage statistics (input/output/total tokens).

- **`std::string GetFinishReason() const`**  
  Returns the completion status (same as the top-level "status" field in JSON).

### Internal Helpers (private)

- **`void Parse()`**  
  Parses the JSON input and fills member data.

- **`void ParseOutput(simdjson::ondemand::array, Reply&)`**
- **`void ParseUsage(simdjson::ondemand::object)`**
- **`void ParseError(simdjson::ondemand::object)`**
- **`ErrorType ParseErrorType(std::string_view type)`**

## Usage Example

```cpp
AIAssistant::ReplyParserAPI2 parser(json_string);

// Check for content
if (parser.HasContent() > 0) {
    std::string response = parser.GetContent();
    // Use the assistant's text output
}

// Check for errors
auto errorType = parser.GetErrorType();
if (errorType != AIAssistant::ReplyParserAPI2::ErrorType::Unknown) {
    auto errorInfo = parser.GetErrorInfo();
    // Handle error
}

// Get usage stats
auto usage = parser.GetUsage(); // AiUsage has input/output/total tokens.
```

## Notes

- Only outputs with type `"output_text"` and non-empty text are returned in `GetContent`.
- Handles and stores only recognized and expected JSON fields, logs unrecognized fields.
- Handles detailed error results, maps error "type" values from the JSON error object to an `ErrorType`.

## License

MIT License (see class header for full terms).

### 56_doc_application_json_replyParserAPI3_h

<a id="56-doc-application-json-replyparserapi3-h"></a>

# ReplyParserAPI3 Class Documentation

**Namespace**: `AIAssistant`  
**Header**: `json/replyParserAPI3.h`  
**Base Class**: `ReplyParser`

## Overview

`ReplyParserAPI3` parses and handles replies from the Google Gemini API (native response format). It extracts response content, usage metadata, completion reasons, and API error information from JSON payloads.  
This class is designed for robust, structured, and efficient extraction of reply information for both human and AI consumption.

## Example JSON Handled

```json
{
  "candidates": [
    {
      "content": {
        "parts": [
          {
            "text": "The engine meets the specified power output requirements."
          }
        ],
        "role": "model"
      },
      "finishReason": "STOP",
      "avgLogprobs": -0.123
    }
  ],
  "usageMetadata": {
    "promptTokenCount": 55,
    "candidatesTokenCount": 17,
    "totalTokenCount": 72
  },
  "modelVersion": "gemini-2.5-flash"
}
```

## Main Structures

### Reply

- **candidates**: list of candidate completions, each with:
  - **content**: 
    - **parts**: text fragments, each with a `text` field.
    - **role**: role origin (e.g., `"model"`).
  - **finishReason**: why the candidate finished (e.g., `"STOP"`, `"MAX_TOKENS"`).

- **usageMetadata**:
  - **promptTokenCount**: tokens in input.
  - **candidatesTokenCount**: tokens in candidates/output.
  - **totalTokenCount**: total tokens processed.

- **modelVersion**: string describing the Gemini model.

### ErrorInfo

- **m_Code**: integer error code.
- **m_Message**: error message string.
- **m_Status**: error status string.

## Public Methods

### Constructor

- `ReplyParserAPI3(const std::string& jsonString)`
  - Initializes and parses the Gemini JSON response string.

### GetErrorInfo

- `const ErrorInfo& GetErrorInfo() const`
  - Returns the parsed error information, if any.

### HasContent

- `size_t HasContent() const override`
  - Returns the number of available candidate responses.

### GetContent

- `std::string GetContent(size_t index = 0) const override`
  - Returns the text content from the candidate at the specified index.
  - Returns an empty string if the index is out of range or content is missing.

### GetError

- `AiError GetError() const override`
  - Returns AI error details, mapped from API error fields.

### GetUsage

- `AiUsage GetUsage() const override`
  - Provides token usage stats from the Gemini API reply.

### GetFinishReason

- `std::string GetFinishReason() const override`
  - Returns the finish reason for the first candidate (e.g., "STOP").

## Internal Parsing

- `Parse()`: parses overall JSON reply, dispatches to handlers for candidates, usage metadata, errors.
- `ParseCandidates(...)`: extracts candidate completions.
- `ParseUsageMetadata(...)`: extracts token usage info.
- `ParseError(...)`: extracts error report details.

## Error Handling

- Sets internal error state on parse failure or Gemini API error in response.
- Logs error details using logging macros.
- Will discard parsed reply if error is detected.

## Usage Example

```cpp
AIAssistant::ReplyParserAPI3 parser(jsonReplyString);
if (parser.HasContent() > 0) {
    std::string content = parser.GetContent();
}
AIAssistant::AiUsage usage = parser.GetUsage();
AIAssistant::AiError err = parser.GetError();
```

## Dependencies

- Inherits from `ReplyParser`.
- Uses [simdjson](https://github.com/simdjson/simdjson) for JSON parsing.
- Utility and logging functions/macros (`LOG_APP_INFO`, `LOG_APP_ERROR`, etc.).

## Summary

`ReplyParserAPI3` efficiently parses Gemini API responses for content, usage, and errors, providing clear access to all important output fields in a structured form. Use it to reliably extract and handle AI reply data in your applications.


---

# ReplyParserAPI4 C++ Class Documentation

## Overview

`ReplyParserAPI4` is a C++ class in the `AIAssistant` namespace that parses Anthropic Claude `/v1/messages` API responses. It extracts message content, usage statistics, finishing reasons, and error details from the JSON response. The class is designed for use with Claude's API responses, which may be either a successful message or an error object.

## Inheritance

- Inherits from `ReplyParser`.

## Usage

```cpp
#include "json/replyParserAPI4.h"

AIAssistant::ReplyParserAPI4 parser(json_string);
if (parser.HasContent() > 0) {
    std::string reply = parser.GetContent();
}
```

## Constructor

- **ReplyParserAPI4(std::string const& jsonString)**
  - Parses the provided JSON string upon construction.

## Public Methods

### Content Access

- **size_t HasContent() const override**
  - Returns the count of text content blocks in the message.
- **std::string GetContent(size_t index = 0) const override**
  - Returns the `text` at the specified content block index. Logs an error and returns empty string if the index is out of range.

### Error Handling

- **AiError GetError() const override**
  - Returns error information. Maps Claude error types to appropriate HTTP status codes.
- **ErrorInfo const& GetErrorInfo() const**
  - Returns reference to internal error information structure.

### Usage Statistics

- **AiUsage GetUsage() const override**
  - Returns token counts (`input_tokens`, `output_tokens`, `total_tokens`).

### Finish Reason

- **std::string GetFinishReason() const override**
  - Returns the reason the API stopped generating.

## Data Structures

### Reply

- **m_Id:** Message identifier (string)
- **m_Model:** Model used (string)
- **m_Content:** List of ContentBlock objects
- **m_StopReason:** Reason for completion (string)
- **m_Usage:** Usage info (Usage struct)

**ContentBlock:**
- **m_Type:** Content type (e.g., `"text"`, `"tool_use"`)
- **m_Text:** Text content

**Usage:**
- **m_InputTokens:** Number of input tokens (uint64_t)
- **m_OutputTokens:** Number of output tokens (uint64_t)

### ErrorInfo

- **m_Type:** Error type (string)
- **m_Message:** Error message (string)

## Parsing Details

- Uses [simdjson](https://simdjson.org/) for fast JSON parsing.
- Supports both success and error response formats from the Claude API.
- On parse errors, populates `ErrorInfo` and marks error state.
- Only content blocks of type `"text"` with non-empty text are considered as content.

## Thread Safety

- Not specified; use external synchronization if required.

## License

- GPL-3.0

## Example API Response Shapes

**Success:**
```json
{
  "id": "msg_xyz",
  "type": "message",
  "role": "assistant",
  "model": "claude-sonnet-4-*",
  "content": [{"type": "text", "text": "..."}],
  "stop_reason": "end_turn" | "max_tokens" | "stop_sequence" | "tool_use",
  "usage": {"input_tokens": N, "output_tokens": M}
}
```

**Error:**
```json
{
  "type": "error",
  "error": {"type": "...", "message": "..."}
}
```

## Dependencies

- `"json/replyParser.h"`
- `simdjson`
- Logging system (`LOG_APP_ERROR` macro)

### 58_doc_application_json_replyParserAPI5_h

# ReplyParserAPI5 C++ Class Documentation

## Overview

`ReplyParserAPI5` is a parser class designed for processing AWS Bedrock model responses. AWS Bedrock returns responses in the native format of each model family (Anthropic, Llama, Titan/Nova). This class detects the model family based on JSON shape and delegates parsing to the appropriate internal parser.

Located in the `AIAssistant` namespace.

## Supported Model Families and JSON Shapes

- **Anthropic-on-Bedrock:**  
  - JSON keys: `{"type": "message", "content": [...], "stop_reason": ...}`
  - Delegate: `ReplyParserAPI4`

- **Llama-on-Bedrock:**  
  - JSON keys: `{"generation": "...", "stop_reason": "...", ...}`
  - Delegate: Internal `LlamaBedrockReply`

- **Titan/Nova-on-Bedrock:**  
  - JSON keys: `{"results": [{"outputText": "...", "completionReason": "..."}], ...}`
  - Delegate: Internal `TitanBedrockReply`

- **Unknown:**  
  - If the JSON does not match any known structure, an error is set.

## Main Functions

### Constructor

```cpp
explicit ReplyParserAPI5(std::string const& jsonString);
```
- Parses the input JSON string and initializes parsing via the appropriate delegate based on detected model family.

### Destructor

```cpp
virtual ~ReplyParserAPI5() = default;
```

### Content Access

- `size_t HasContent() const override`:  
  Returns 1 if the reply contains content, 0 otherwise.

- `std::string GetContent(size_t index = 0) const override`:  
  Returns the reply content at the specified index (indexing only used for Anthropic; others return index 0 always).

### Error and Usage Handling

- `AiError GetError() const override`:  
  Returns the parsing or model error, if any.

- `AiUsage GetUsage() const override`:  
  Returns usage statistics (input, output, and total tokens), if available.

- `std::string GetFinishReason() const override`:  
  Returns a string summarizing why generation was stopped.

## Design Details

- Uses simdjson for fast JSON parsing.
- Selection of the correct parser is automatic based on the presence of unique JSON keys.
- Error handling is consistently reported via `GetError()`; unknown/invalid shapes fill error details.

## Internal Classes

- **LlamaBedrockReply**: Parses Llama model replies.
- **TitanBedrockReply**: Parses Titan/Nova model replies.
- **ReplyParserAPI4**: Delegated for Anthropic family replies (external).

## Example Usage

```cpp
std::string json = ... // Response from Bedrock
AIAssistant::ReplyParserAPI5 parser(json);
if (parser.HasContent()) {
    std::string output = parser.GetContent();
    // Use output
} else {
    auto err = parser.GetError();
    // Handle error
}
```

## License

GPL-3.0  
© 2026 JC Technolabs

### 59_doc_application_json_requestBuilder_h

# AIAssistant::IRequestBuilder Class Documentation

## Overview

`IRequestBuilder` is an abstract base class interface for constructing HTTP requests for different AI model APIs. Implementations of this interface generate appropriate JSON bodies and URLs, handle authentication styles, and provide feature flags for various APIs.

This class is designed for use in AI-assistant workflows where requests must be constructed according to the particular requirements of each API or model provider.

---

## Key Responsibilities

- Build request bodies in JSON format for different AI endpoints
- Generate correct endpoint URLs
- Specify the required authentication method per API
- (Optionally) provide extra request headers
- Indicate support for features such as native JSON schema and tool shims

---

## Member Functions

### Destructor

- `virtual ~IRequestBuilder() = default;`  
  Virtual destructor for safe polymorphic use.

---

### Core Interface

#### Request Body Construction

- `virtual std::string BuildBody(const AiInvocation& envelope, const std::string& model) const = 0;`  
  Build and return the JSON request body using the invocation context and model.

#### URL Resolution

- `virtual std::string ResolveUrl(const std::string& baseUrl, const std::string& model) const = 0;`  
  Return the full endpoint URL, optionally including the model.

#### Authentication Style

- `virtual CurlWrapper::AuthStyle GetAuthStyle() const = 0;`  
  Return the required authentication style for this API (e.g., Bearer token, API key, AWS SigV4, etc.).

#### Extra Headers

- `virtual std::unordered_map<std::string, std::string> GetExtraHeaders() const`  
  Return a map of additional headers (default: empty).

---

#### Feature Flags

- `virtual bool SupportsNativeJsonSchema() const`  
  Return true if the API natively supports JSON schema inputs/outputs (default: false).

- `virtual bool SupportsForcedToolShim() const`  
  Return true if forced tool shimming is supported (default: false).

---

### Static Factory

- `static std::unique_ptr<IRequestBuilder> Create(ConfigParser::EngineConfig::InterfaceType interfaceType);`  
  Factory function to instantiate the correct request builder for the given API interface type.

---

## Provided Implementations

The following derived classes implement specific API request formats and behaviors:

- **RequestBuilderAPI1**: Simple JSON body; Bearer auth.
- **RequestBuilderAPI6**: Azure (API1 body/URL, but uses `api-key` auth).
- **RequestBuilderAPI2**: JSON body for APIs expecting "input" instead of "messages".
- **RequestBuilderAPI3**: Google Gemini style; nested parts and contents; X-Goog-API-Key auth.
- **RequestBuilderAPI4**: Anthropic-style body; explicit max_tokens; X-API-key auth.
- **RequestBuilderAPI5**: AWS Bedrock; routes to Anthropic, Llama, Titan/Nova body formats by model family; AWS SigV4 auth.

Selection is based on `ConfigParser::EngineConfig::InterfaceType`.

---

## Usage Example

```cpp
auto builder = AIAssistant::IRequestBuilder::Create(interfaceType);
std::string body = builder->BuildBody(aiInvocation, modelName);
std::string url = builder->ResolveUrl(baseUrl, modelName);
CurlWrapper::AuthStyle auth = builder->GetAuthStyle();
// Use these results to make an HTTP request
```

---

## Related Dependencies

- `AiInvocation`: Holds invocation context (messages, settings, etc.)
- `CurlWrapper::AuthStyle`: Enum defining HTTP authentication schemas
- `ConfigParser::EngineConfig::InterfaceType`: Enum specifying API type
- `JsonHelper`: Performs JSON sanitization

---

## License

GPL-3.0  
Copyright (c) 2026 JC Technolabs

---

## Notes

- This class is not intended for direct instantiation; use the static `Create` method.
- Implementation selection and future expansion are supported by the interface and factory approach.
- For details on derived class behavior, see the corresponding implementation in the source code.


---

### 60_doc_application_json_schemaValidator_h

<a id="60-doc-application-json-schemavalidator-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/60_doc_application_json_schemaValidator_h/docu.output.md`



# AIAssistant::SchemaValidator

## Overview

`SchemaValidator` is a C++ class for validating JSON documents against a subset of [JSON Schema Draft 2020-12](https://json-schema.org/) using [simdjson](https://github.com/simdjson/simdjson). The class checks JSON structure and types, enforcing constraints defined in a schema.

Unsupported keywords (e.g., `allOf`, `not`, `format`, etc.) cause schema loading to fail with a clear error message.

## Supported JSON Schema Keywords

- `type` (string | number | integer | boolean | object | array | null)
- `properties`
- `required`
- `additionalProperties`
- `items`
- `enum`
- `minimum`, `maximum`
- `minLength`, `maxLength`
- `pattern`
- `oneOf`, `anyOf`
- `$ref`, `$defs`

Unsupported or unknown schema keywords are logged or rejected at load time.

## Class Structure

### ValidationError

Error instance describing a failed validation.

| Member         | Type        | Description                                  |
| -------------- | ---------- | --------------------------------------------- |
| `m_Path`       | `std::string` | JSON Pointer to the failing value.         |
| `m_Message`    | `std::string` | Description of the validation error.       |

---

### ValidationResult

Result object from validation.

| Member         | Type                         | Description                |
| -------------- | ----------------------------|---------------------------|
| `m_Ok`         | `bool`                      | True if validation passed. |
| `m_Errors`     | `std::vector<ValidationError>` | List of all errors.     |

---

### SchemaValidator

Main class used for loading a schema and validating JSON documents.

#### Construction & Lifetime

- `explicit SchemaValidator(std::string schemaJson);`
  - Loads the provided schema. Schemas using unsupported keywords are rejected.
- Destructor cleans up resources.

#### Deleted Operations

- Copy and assignment are **deleted** (not allowed).

#### Methods

| Method                                                                              | Description                                              |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| `bool IsLoaded() const;`                                                            | Returns true if schema loaded successfully.              |
| `std::string const& LoadError() const;`                                             | Returns load error if schema could not be loaded.        |
| `ValidationResult Validate(std::string const& documentJson) const;`                 | Validates a JSON document against the loaded schema.     |
| `static std::string FormatErrorsForModel(std::vector<ValidationError> const& errors);` | Formats errors for display, e.g., for LLMs or humans.    |

#### Example Usage

```cpp
// Load schema
std::string schema = R"({
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "name": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1 },
    "age": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 0 }
  },
  "required": ["name"]
})";
AIAssistant::SchemaValidator validator(schema);

if (!validator.IsLoaded()) {
    std::cerr << "Load error: " << validator.LoadError() << std::endl;
    return;
}

std::string json = R"({ "name": "Alice", "age": 30 })";
auto result = validator.Validate(json);

if (!result.m_Ok) {
    std::cout << "Validation failed:\n"
              << AIAssistant::SchemaValidator::FormatErrorsForModel(result.m_Errors)
              << std::endl;
}
```

## Error Handling

- If schema loading fails, `IsLoaded()` returns false and `LoadError()` contains details.
- On validation failure, `ValidationResult.m_Ok` is false and errors detail all failing locations/constraints.
- Unsupported schema features are detected at load time, not during validation.

## Implementation Notes

- Uses [simdjson](https://github.com/simdjson/simdjson) for fast parsing.
- Internal implementation uses PImpl idiom to hide details.
- Only the JSON Schema 2020-12 subset listed above is supported.
- Extensively checks for types, required fields, allowed properties, array and value constraints, and string/number limits.

## License

GPL-3.0.  
See source comments for full text.

## Namespace

All functionality is in `AIAssistant`.

---

**Summary:**  
`SchemaValidator` allows C++ code to efficiently check if JSON documents match a provided schema, reporting all constraint violations in a detailed and structured way. Unsupported features are rejected up front for safety and clarity.


---

### 61_doc_application_log_statusRenderer_h

<a id="61-doc-application-log-statusrenderer-h"></a>

*Source doc:* `../../../queue/jarvisCppDocu/61_doc_application_log_statusRenderer_h/docu.output.md`



# StatusRenderer Class Documentation

## Overview

`AIAssistant::StatusRenderer` is a C++ class for rendering real-time status updates, combining information from various components of an AI assistant system for display in terminal UIs (TUI) and web views. It ensures the status output is synchronized between the backend and all front-end views.

## Key Responsibilities

- Provides up-to-date information (snapshots) about system status, workflows, keys, cloud connectors, and Python integration.
- Organizes status into two textual rows for display.
- Handles truncated UTF-8 strings for fixed-width layouts.
- Supports injecting custom providers to query live status and workflow summaries.
- Designed for lightweight, frequent updates (e.g., UI redraws).

## Main Components

### 1. `RuntimeSnapshot`
A structure describing current system state:

| Field             | Type      | Description                                   |
|-------------------|-----------|-----------------------------------------------|
| `keysStatus`      | string    | Key status: "ok", "no_password", "wrong_password", "no_keys_file" |
| `hasProviders`    | bool      | At least one provider loaded                  |
| `aiInflight`      | size_t    | Count of in-flight AI queries                 |
| `activeRuns`      | size_t    | Number of active workflow runs                |
| `mcpConnected`    | bool      | MCP sidecar connection status                 |
| `cloudHealthy`    | size_t    | Healthy cloud connectors                      |
| `cloudRecovering` | size_t    | Recovering cloud connectors                   |
| `cloudOpen`       | size_t    | "Circuit open" (failed) cloud connectors      |
| `totalCompleted`  | uint64_t  | Total completed workflow runs                 |
| `totalFailed`     | uint64_t  | Total failed workflow runs                    |
| `pythonRunning`   | bool      | Python integration health                     |

### 2. `LastRunSummary`
A structure summarizing recent workflow execution:

| Field            | Type      | Description                                     |
|------------------|-----------|-------------------------------------------------|
| `displayId`      | string    | Short ID for referencing the workflow run       |
| `state`          | string    | Workflow state ("succeeded", "failed", etc.)    |
| `completedAtIso` | string    | ISO-8601 UTC completion timestamp (empty if still running) |
| `isAdhoc`        | bool      | Indicates if the run was ad hoc                 |

### 3. Provider Types
- **RuntimeSnapshotProvider:**  
  `std::function<RuntimeSnapshot()>`  
  Function returning the latest `RuntimeSnapshot`.

- **LastRunsProvider:**  
  `std::function<std::vector<LastRunSummary>(size_t maxCount)>`  
  Function returning up to `maxCount` recent runs.

## Main API

### Constructor

```cpp
StatusRenderer();
```
Default constructor; does not automatically start/stop.

### Providers

- `void SetRuntimeSnapshotProvider(RuntimeSnapshotProvider provider);`
  - Registers a function to supply runtime status.
- `void SetLastRunsProvider(LastRunsProvider provider);`
  - Registers a function to supply the latest run summaries.

### Operation

- `void Start();`
  - Placeholder. No operation in this header.
- `void Stop();`
  - Placeholder. No operation in this header.

### Status Output

- `void BuildStatusLines(std::vector<std::string>& outLines, int maxColumns);`
  - Fills `outLines` (always two strings) with:
    - **Row 1:** System/connection/keys/cloud status, totals, indicators.
    - **Row 2:** "Last runs" summary (up to 3), keys-locked warning if applicable.
  - Output lines are truncated to `maxColumns` columns, preserving UTF-8 integrity.

- `size_t GetRowCount();`
  - Always returns `2` (lines outputted per build).

## Behavior Details

- Status lines use colored glyphs/indicators for clear UI feedback.
- Includes "spinner" glyph when AI queries are active.
- If master keys are locked or providers are missing, displays unlock/setup hints.
- All provider functions should be lightweight, as they are called once per redraw.
- Thread safety is ensured around provider set/get.

## Usage Example

```cpp
AIAssistant::StatusRenderer renderer;
renderer.SetRuntimeSnapshotProvider(GetSnapshot);
renderer.SetLastRunsProvider(GetRecentRuns);

std::vector<std::string> lines;
renderer.BuildStatusLines(lines, 120);

for (const auto& line : lines) {
    std::cout << line << std::endl;
}
```

## Concurrency

- Internally synchronized with `std::mutex` to protect provider assignment and internal state.

## Notes

- UI does not permit unlocking keys; prompts user to use dashboard for key management.
- Designed for both web and terminal-based dashboards.
- Extensive comments in code match dashboard signal semantics for consistent multi-UI experience.
- The `Start()` and `Stop()` methods are placeholders and can be ignored unless implementation is extended.

## License

MIT License (2025 JC Technolabs). See code header for full terms.
